import "./globals.css";
import { ReactNode } from "react";

import { AppProviders } from "@/components/providers/app-providers";
import { LanguageProvider } from "@/lib/i18n/context";

export const metadata = {
  title: "CMEP · Obehové materiály",
  description: "Circular Materials Exchange Platform frontend"
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="sk">
      <body>
        <AppProviders>
          <LanguageProvider>{children}</LanguageProvider>
        </AppProviders>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

                <p className="ff mb-2 text-2xl" style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>Pre planétu</p>
                <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                  Menej odpadu, viac obehu, viac času.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="ff mt-10 text-2xl italic leading-tight md:text-3xl" style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>
              CMEP — pretože čistota nie je luxus. Je to zodpovednosť.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* ČO SA TU HÝBE */}
        <section id="trh" className="border-t border-[color:color-mix(in_srgb,#c9a84c_15%,transparent)]">

              {MATERIALS.map((m) => (

                  <div className="relative aspect-[16/10] w-full">
                    <img src={m.img} alt={m.label} className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-60 transition group-hover:opacity-80 group-hover:scale-[1.02]" />
                    <div className="grad absolute inset-0" />

                      <p className="ff text-3xl" style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>{m.label}</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>{m.desc}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* STRATA */}
        <section className="border-t border-[color:color-mix(in_srgb,#c9a84c_15%,transparent)]">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-16 md:py-20">
            <p className="mb-4 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>STRATA, KTORÚ UŽ POČÍTATE</p>
            <h2 className="ff text-4xl leading-tight md:text-5xl">
              Každá tona na skládke je peniaz, o ktorý ste prišli.
            </h2>
            <p className="ff mt-6 text-3xl italic md:text-4xl" style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>
              300 t pilín. Žilina. Dnes.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-6 max-w-2xl text-base leading-relaxed" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
              Piliny v Žiline. 300 t. Dnes. Kým dočítate túto vetu, niekto ich kúpi — alebo skončia v kotli.
              Burza existuje preto, aby sa to nestalo zo zotrvačnosti.
            </p>
            <Link href="/dashboard" className="mt-8 inline-block rounded-md px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold" style={{ background: GOLD, color: BG }}>
              Do tržnice
            </Link>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* AKO TO FUNGUJE */}
        <section className="border-t border-[color:color-mix(in_srgb,#c9a84c_15%,transparent)]">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-16 md:py-20">
            <p className="mb-4 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>AKO TO FUNGUJE</p>
            <h2 className="ff mb-10 text-4xl leading-tight md:text-5xl">Tri kroky. Od prebytku k obchodu za pár minút.</h2>
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
              {STEPS.map((s) => (
                <div key={s.n} className="rounded-2xl border p-6 md:p-8" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)' }}>
                  <p className="ff text-5xl" style={{ color: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 60%, transparent)' }}>{s.n}</p>
                  <p className="mt-4 text-2xl" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{s.icon}</p>

        <section className="border-t border-[color:color-mix(in_srgb,#c9a84c_15%,transparent)]">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 md:py-20 text-center">
            <p className="mb-4 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>SÚKROMIE</p>
            <h2 className="ff text-4xl leading-tight md:text-5xl">
              Kontakt príde ako správa.
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-6 text-base leading-relaxed md:text-lg" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
              Telefón a e-mail ostanú skryté, kým sa firmy nedohodnú.
              Žiadny spam, žiadne obvolávanie. Súkromie je súčasť ceny.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* TICHÝ VSTUP */}
        <section className="border-t border-[color:color-mix(in_srgb,#c9a84c_15%,transparent)]">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 md:py-20">
            <p className="mb-4 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>TICHÝ VSTUP</p>
            <h2 className="ff text-4xl leading-tight md:text-5xl">Nemusíte sa registrovať, aby ste sa opýtali.</h2>
            <p className="mt-6 text-base" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
              Jedna správa stačí. Ozveme sa ako firma firme — nie ako newsletter.
            </p>
            {sent ? (
              <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border p-6" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 35%, transparent)', background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.05)' }}>
                <p className="ff text-xl" style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>Ďakujeme. Ozveme sa.</p>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>Vaša správa je u nás. Odpovieme do 24 hodín.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={onContact} className="mt-8 grid gap-3">
                <input required value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} placeholder="Meno" className="h-12 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-4 outline-none" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }} />
                <input required type="email" value={form.email} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })} placeholder="E-mail" className="h-12 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-4 outline-none" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }} />
                <input value={form.company} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, company: e.target.value })} placeholder="Firma" className="h-12 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-4 outline-none" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }} />
                <textarea required value={form.message} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, message: e.target.value })} placeholder="Správa" rows={5} className="rounded-md border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 outline-none" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }} />
                <button type="submit" className="justify-self-start rounded-md px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold" style={{ background: GOLD, color: BG }}>
                  Odoslať
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* FOOTER */}
        <footer className="border-t border-[color:color-mix(in_srgb,#c9a84c_15%,transparent)]">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-12">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <img src="/trc-mark.jpg" alt="CMEP" className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full object-cover" />
                <div>
                  <p className="ff text-2xl">CMEP</p>
                  <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: MUTED }}>Circular Materials Exchange Platform</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>Súkromná burza. Overené firmy. Kontakty, ktoré patria vám.</p>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-2 gap-6">
              <div>
                <p className="mb-3 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>TRH</p>
                <Link href="/dashboard" className="block py-1 text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>Trhovisko</Link>
                <Link href="/dashboard" className="block py-1 text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>Firmy</Link>
                <Link href="#preco" className="block py-1 text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>O platforme</Link>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="mb-3 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>DÔVERA</p>
                <span className="block py-1 text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>Overené IČO</span>
                <span className="block py-1 text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>Skryté kontakty</span>
                <span className="block py-1 text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>Súkromná burza</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-10 flex items-center justify-between border-t pt-6 text-xs" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 15%, transparent)', color: FAINT }}>
              <span>© CMEP · Circular Materials Exchange Platform · Slovensko</span>
              <span>JAZYK: SK</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </footer>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { config } from '@/lib/config';
import { tokenStore } from '@/lib/auth/token-store';

function AdminReports() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = tokenStore.getAccessToken?.() || tokenStore.getToken?.();
    fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}/events/reports`, {
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
    })
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d) => setItems(d.items || []))
      .catch(() => setItems([]));
  }, []);
  if (!items.length) return <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#9a8f7c]">Ziadne nahlasenia.</p>;
  return (
    <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-[#f3ead8]">
      {items.map((it) => (
        <li key={it.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#d4af37]/20 p-3">
          <p>{it.payload || it.eventType}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#9a8f7c]">{it.createdAt} · user {it.actorUserId}</p>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useAuthRequired, useIsPlatformAdmin } from '@/features/auth';
import { useAdminStats } from '@/features/admin';
import { AdminUsersTable, AuditTable } from '@/features/admin';
import { CompaniesTable } from '@/features/companies';
import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { DashboardFooter } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-footer';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { useT } from '@/lib/i18n/context';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const BG = '#090807';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';

export default function AdminPage() {
  const { user, isLoading } = useAuthRequired();
  const isPlatformAdmin = useIsPlatformAdmin();
  const { data: stats, isLoading: isLoadingStats } = useAdminStats();
  const { t } = useT();

  const safeStats = (stats || {}) as {
    total_users?: number;
    total_listings?: number;
    total_companies?: number;
    pending_verifications?: number;
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG }}>
        <DashboardHeader />
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-16 text-center" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
          {t('admin.loading')}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (user && !isPlatformAdmin) {
    redirect('/dashboard');
  }

  return (
    <>
      <link
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,500&family=Outfit:wght@400;500;600&display=swap"
        rel="stylesheet"
      />
      <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG, fontFamily: 'Outfit, sans-serif' }}>
        <DashboardHeader />

        <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-10">
          <p className="mb-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
            {t('admin.kicker')}
          </p>
          <h1 className="mb-10 text-4xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em] md:text-5xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
            {t('admin.title')}
          </h1>

          {/* ŠTATISTIKY */}
          <section className="mb-12 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
            <StatCard label="Používatelia" value={safeStats.total_users} loading={isLoadingStats} />
            <StatCard label="Ponuky" value={safeStats.total_listings} loading={isLoadingStats} />
            <StatCard label="Firmy" value={safeStats.total_companies} loading={isLoadingStats} />
            <StatCard label="{t('admin.pending')}" value={safeStats.pending_verifications} loading={isLoadingStats} accent />
          </section>

          {/* {t('admin.firmsKicker')} NA OVERENIE */}
          <section className="mb-12">
            <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
              {t('admin.firmsKicker')}
            </p>
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-2xl md:text-3xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
              {t('admin.firmsTitle')}
            </h2>
            <p className="mb-6 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
              Firmy, ktoré požiadali o overenie. Klikni <strong style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>Overiť</strong> pre schválenie alebo <strong style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>Zamietnuť</strong> pre odmietnutie.
            </p>
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border p-5"
              style={{
                borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)',
                background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.03)',
              }}
            >
              <CompaniesTable />
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* {t('admin.usersKicker')} */}
          <section className="mb-12">
            <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
              {t('admin.usersKicker')}
            </p>
            <h2 className="mb-6 text-2xl md:text-3xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
              {t('admin.usersTitle')}
            </h2>
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border p-5"
              style={{
                borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)',
                background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.03)',
              }}
            >
              <AdminUsersTable />
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* AUDIT {t('admin.historyKicker')} */}
          <section className="mb-12">
            <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
              {t('admin.historyKicker')}
            </p>
            <h2 className="mb-6 text-2xl md:text-3xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
              {t('admin.historyTitle')}
            </h2>
            <p className="mb-6 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
              {t('admin.historyHint')}
            </p>
            <AuditTable />
          </section>

        
      <section className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/25 p-4">
        <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Nahlasenia</p>
        <AdminReports />
      </section>
    </main>

      <DashboardFooter />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  loading,
  accent = false,
}: {
  label: string;
  value?: number;
  loading: boolean;
  accent?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className="rounded-2xl border p-5"
      style={{
        borderColor: accent
          ? 'color-mix(in srgb, #c45c4a 40%, transparent)'
          : 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)',
        background: accent ? 'rgba(196,92,74,0.05)' : 'rgba(201,168,76,0.03)',
      }}
    >
      <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
        {label}
      </p>
      <p
        className="mt-2 text-3xl md:text-4xl"
        style={{
          fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif',
          color: accent ? '#e8a598' : GOLD_BRIGHT,
        }}
      >
        {loading ? '…' : (value ?? 0)}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useAuthRequired, useIsPlatformAdmin } from '@/features/auth';
import { useAdminStats } from '@/features/admin';
import { AdminUsersTable } from '@/features/admin';
import { CompaniesTable } from '@/features/companies';
import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { DashboardFooter } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-footer';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const BG = '#090807';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';

export default function AdminPage() {
  const { user, isLoading } = useAuthRequired();
  const isPlatformAdmin = useIsPlatformAdmin();
  const { data: stats, isLoading: isLoadingStats } = useAdminStats();

  const safeStats = (stats || {}) as {
    total_users?: number;
    total_listings?: number;
    total_companies?: number;
    pending_verifications?: number;
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG }}>
        <DashboardHeader />
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-16 text-center" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
          Načítavam…
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (user && !isPlatformAdmin) {
    redirect('/dashboard');
  }

  return (
    <>
      <link
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,500&family=Outfit:wght@400;500;600&display=swap"
        rel="stylesheet"

            Administrácia systému
          </h1>

          {/* ŠTATISTIKY */}
          <section className="mb-12 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
            <StatCard label="Používatelia" value={safeStats.total_users} loading={isLoadingStats} />
            <StatCard label="Ponuky" value={safeStats.total_listings} loading={isLoadingStats} />
            <StatCard label="Firmy" value={safeStats.total_companies} loading={isLoadingStats} />
            <StatCard label="Čakajúce overenia" value={safeStats.pending_verifications} loading={isLoadingStats} accent />
          </section>

          {/* FIRMY NA OVERENIE */}
          <section className="mb-12">
            <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
              FIRMY
            </p>
            <h2 className="mb-3 text-2xl md:text-3xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
              Firmy na overenie
            </h2>
            <p className="mb-6 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
              Firmy, ktoré požiadali o overenie. Klikni <strong style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>Overiť</strong> pre schválenie alebo <strong style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>Zamietnuť</strong> pre odmietnutie.
            </p>
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border p-5"
              style={{
                borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)',
                background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.03)',
              }}
            >
              <CompaniesTable />
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* POUŽÍVATELIA */}
          <section className="mb-12">
            <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
              POUŽÍVATELIA
            </p>
            <h2 className="mb-6 text-2xl md:text-3xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
              Správa používateľov

FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/admin/recommendations/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { config } from '@/lib/config';
import { getListingRecommendations } from '@/features/recommendations/api';

type ListingRow = { id: string; title: string; materialType?: string; location?: string };

async function fetchListings(): Promise<ListingRow[]> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/listings', {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
  });
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Listings request failed');
  const rows = (body.items || body.data || body) as ListingRow[];
  return Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
}

export default function AdminRecommendationsPage() {
  const listingsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: ['admin-listings'], queryFn: fetchListings });
  const firstId = listingsQuery.data?.[0]?.id;
  const recQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['admin-rec', firstId],
    queryFn: () => getListingRecommendations(firstId as string),
    enabled: !!firstId,
  });

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl bg-[#070707] p-6 text-[#f3e6c4]">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Admin</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-3xl">Odporucania</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Prehlad match-ov. Ziadny auto-deal.</p>
      {listingsQuery.error && <p className="mt-4 text-red-300">{(listingsQuery.error as Error).message}</p>}
      <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
        {(listingsQuery.data || []).map((row) => (
          <li key={row.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#d4af37]/25 p-3">
            <a href={'/dashboard/listings/' + row.id} className="text-[#d4af37]">{row.title || row.id}</a>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#9a8b63]">{row.materialType} · {row.location}</p>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {recQuery.data && (
        <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 p-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Prva ponuka: {recQuery.data.items.length} kandidatov</p>
        </section>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/chat/[id]/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { DashboardFooter } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-footer';
import { useMessages, useSendMessage, useMarkRead } from '@/features/chat';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const BG = '#090807';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';

type Message = {
  id: string;
  body?: string;
  senderUserId?: string;
  sender_user_id?: string;
  createdAt?: string;
  created_at?: string;
};

export default function ChatThreadPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const id = String(params?.id || '');
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const bottomRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const { data, isLoading } = useMessages(id);
  const sendMutation = useSendMessage(id);
  const markReadMutation = useMarkRead();

  // Auto-scroll na koniec pri nových správach
  useEffect(() => {
    bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [data]);

  // Mark as read pri otvorení
  useEffect(() => {
    if (id) {
      markReadMutation.mutate(id);
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [id]);

  const raw: any = data;
  const items: Message[] = Array.isArray(raw)
    ? raw
    : raw?.items ?? raw?.data?.items ?? raw?.data ?? [];

  const onSend = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const body = draft.trim();
    if (!body) return;
    setErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      await sendMutation.mutateAsync(body);
      setDraft('');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setErrorMsg(err?.message || 'Odoslanie zlyhalo');
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <link
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,500&family=Outfit:wght@400;500;600&display=swap"
        rel="stylesheet"
      />
      <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG, fontFamily: 'Outfit, sans-serif' }}>
        <DashboardHeader />

        <main className="mx-auto flex max-w-4xl flex-col px-4 py-10">
          {/* BREADCRUMB */}
          <nav className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm" aria-label="Navigácia">
            <Link
              href="/dashboard"
              className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}
            >
              ← Dashboard
            </Link>
            <span style={{ color: MUTED }}>/</span>
            <Link
              href="/dashboard/chat"
              className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}
            >
              Chat
            </Link>
          </nav>
          <p className="mb-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
            KONVERZÁCIA
          </p>
          <h1 className="mb-8 text-3xl md:text-4xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
            Chat
          </h1>

          <div
            className="flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border p-5"
            style={{
              borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)',
              minHeight: '50vh',
              maxHeight: '65vh',
            }}
          >
            {isLoading ? (
              <p style={{ color: MUTED }}>Načítavam…</p>
            ) : items.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-center text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                Zatiaľ žiadne správy.
              </p>
            ) : (
              items.map((m) => {
                const body = m.body ?? '';
                const at = m.createdAt ?? m.created_at;
                const sender = m.senderUserId ?? m.sender_user_id ?? '';
                return (
                  <div
                    key={m.id}
                    className="rounded-xl border p-3"
                    style={{
                      borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 15%, transparent)',
                      background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.03)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                      <span style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>{sender.slice(0, 8)}…</span>
                      {at && <span>{new Date(at).toLocaleString()}</span>}
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>
                      {body}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
            <div ref={bottomRef} />
          </div>

          {errorMsg && (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm" style={{ color: '#e8a598' }}>
              {errorMsg}
            </p>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={onSend} className="mt-4 grid gap-3">
            <textarea
              value={draft}
              onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Napíš správu…"
              rows={3}
              disabled={sendMutation.isPending}
              className="rounded-md border bg-transparent px-4 py-3 outline-none disabled:opacity-50"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }}
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={sendMutation.isPending || !draft.trim()}
              className="justify-self-start rounded-md px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50"
              style={{ background: GOLD, color: BG }}
            >
              {sendMutation.isPending ? 'Odosielam…' : 'Odoslať'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </main>

        <DashboardFooter />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

'use client';
import { ReportButton } from '@/features/recommendations/components/report-button';
import { ChatDraftBanner } from '@/features/recommendations/components/chat-draft-banner';

import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { DashboardFooter } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-footer';
import { useT } from '@/lib/i18n/context';
import { useThreads } from '@/features/chat';
import Link from 'next/link';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const BG = '#090807';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';
const FAINT = '#6e6658';

type Thread = {
  id: string;
  lastMessage?: string | null;
  unreadCount?: number;
  subject?: string;
};

export default function ChatPage() {
  const { t } = useT();
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useThreads();

  const raw: any = data;
  const items: Thread[] = Array.isArray(raw)
    ? raw
    : raw?.items ?? raw?.data?.items ?? raw?.data ?? [];

  return (

    <>
      <link
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,500&family=Outfit:wght@400;500;600&display=swap"
        rel="stylesheet"
      />
      <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG, fontFamily: 'Outfit, sans-serif' }}>
        <DashboardHeader />
      <ChatDraftBanner />
      <ReportButton />

        <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-10">
          <p className="mb-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
            {t('chat.section')}
          </p>
          <h1
            className="mb-10 text-4xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em] md:text-5xl"
            style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}
          >
            {t('chat.title')}
          </h1>

          {isLoading ? (
            <p style={{ color: MUTED }}>{t('chat.loading')}</p>
          ) : error ? (
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border p-5 text-sm"
              style={{ borderColor: '#c45c4a', color: '#e8a598' }}
            >
              Chyba pri načítaní konverzácií.
            </div>
          ) : items.length === 0 ? (
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border py-16 text-center"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)' }}
            >
              <h3 className="text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
                {t('chat.empty')}
              </h3>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                {t('chat.emptyHint')}
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="space-y-3">
              {items.map((thread) => {
                const unread = Number(thread.unreadCount || 0);
                return (
                  <li key={thread.id}>
                    <Link
                      href={`/dashboard/chat/${thread.id}`}
                      className="block rounded-2xl border p-5 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5"
                      style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)' }}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <p
                            className="text-lg"
                            style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}
                          >
                            {thread.subject || t('chat.firm')}
                          </p>
                          <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-1 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                            {thread.lastMessage || '—'}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        {unread > 0 && (
                          <span
                            className="rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold"
                            style={{ background: '#c45c4a', color: FG }}
                          >
                            {unread}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          )}
        </main>

        <DashboardFooter />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/companies/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { config } from '@/lib/config';
import { useT } from '@/lib/i18n/context';

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

type Company = {
  id: string;
  legalName?: string;
  city?: string;
  status?: string;
  acceptedMaterialTypes?: string | null;
};

function cleanFilter(raw: string): string {
  return raw.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9áäčďéíľĺňóôŕšťúýžÁÄČĎÉÍĽĹŇÓÔŔŠŤÚÝŽ,.\-\s]/g, '').slice(0, 64);
}

async function fetchCompanies(page: number): Promise<{ items: Company[]; total: number }> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/companies?page=' + page + '&pageSize=' + PAGE_SIZE, {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
  });
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(typeof body.detail === 'string' ? body.detail : 'HTTP ' + res.status);
  const items = (body.items || body.data || []) as Company[];
  const total = Number(body.meta?.total ?? body.total ?? items.length);
  return { items: Array.isArray(items) ? items : [], total };
}

export default function CompaniesPage() {
  const { t } = useT();
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [material, setMaterial] = useState('');
  const { data, error, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['companies', page],
    queryFn: () => fetchCompanies(page),
  });
  const filter = cleanFilter(material).trim().toLowerCase();
  const items = (data?.items || []).filter((row) =>
    filter ? (row.acceptedMaterialTypes || '').toLowerCase().includes(filter) : true,
  );
  const total = data?.total || 0;
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE));

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#090807] text-[#f3ead8]">
      <DashboardHeader />
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl p-6">
        <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#c9a84c]">{t('companies.kicker')}</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-3xl">{t('companies.title')}</h1>
        <Link href="/dashboard/companies/new" className="mt-3 inline-block text-sm text-[#c9a84c]">
          {t('companies.add')}
        </Link>
        <input
          value={material}
          onChange={(e) => setMaterial(cleanFilter(e.target.value))}
          placeholder={t('companies.filter')}
          maxLength={64}
          className="mt-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#c9a84c]/25 bg-black p-2 text-sm"
        />
        {isLoading && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#9a8f7c]">{t('companies.loading')}</p>}
        {error && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-red-300">{(error as Error).message}</p>}
        <ul className="mt-6 space-y-3">
          {items.map((row) => (
            <li key={row.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-[#c9a84c]/25 p-4">
              <p className="font-semibold">{row.legalName || row.id}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-[#9a8f7c]">{row.city} · {row.status}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#9a8f7c]">
                {t('companies.materials')}: {row.acceptedMaterialTypes || t('companies.empty')}
              </p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
          <button type="button" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)} className="text-[#c9a84c] disabled:text-[#6e6658]">
            {t('companies.prev')}
          </button>
          <span>{page} / {pages}</span>
          <button type="button" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)} className="text-[#c9a84c] disabled:text-[#6e6658]">
            {t('companies.next')}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/listings/[id]/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { config } from '../../../../lib/config';
import { RecommendationsPanel } from '../../../../features/recommendations/components/recommendations-panel';
import { TransportPanel } from '../../../../features/recommendations/components/transport-panel';
import { FreightPanel } from '../../../../features/recommendations/components/freight-panel';
import { postListingViewed } from '../../../../features/recommendations/api';
import { ListingInquiryButton } from '../../../../features/inquiries/components/listing-inquiry-button';

type ListingData = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  materialType: string;
  quantity: number;
  unit: string;
  location: string;
  status: string;
  description?: string | null;
};

async function fetchListing(listingId: string): Promise<ListingData> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/listings/' + listingId, {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
  });
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) {
    const detail = typeof body.detail === 'string' ? body.detail : 'Listing request failed';
    throw new Error(detail);
  }
  return (body.data ?? body) as ListingData;
}

export default function ListingDetailPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const listingId = params?.id;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (listingId) {
      void postListingViewed(listingId);
    }
  }, [listingId]);
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState('');
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['listing', listingId],
    queryFn: () => fetchListing(listingId as string),
    enabled: !!listingId,
    retry: 1,
  });

  if (!listingId) return <p className="p-6 text-[#d4af37]">Chyba ID ponuky.</p>;

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl bg-[#070707] p-6 text-[#f3e6c4]">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Ponuka</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-3xl">{data?.title || 'Ponuka'}</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs text-[#9a8b63]">{listingId}</p>
      {isLoading && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Nacitavam ponuku…</p>}
      {error && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-red-300">{(error as Error).message}</p>}
      {data && (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 bg-black p-5 text-sm">
          <p>Material: {data.materialType}</p>
          <p>Mnozstvo: {data.quantity} {data.unit}</p>
          <p>Lokalita: {data.location}</p>
          <p>Stav: {data.status}</p>
          {data.description ? <p className="mt-2 text-[#cbb98a]">{data.description}</p> : null}
          <ListingInquiryButton listingId={listingId} listingTitle={data.title} />
        </div>
      )}
      <RecommendationsPanel
        listingId={listingId}
        listingTitle={data?.title}
        listingLocation={data?.location}
        materialType={data?.materialType}
        onPickDestination={setDestination}
      />
      <TransportPanel origin={data?.location} destination={destination} />
      <FreightPanel near={destination || data?.location} />
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/listings/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { DashboardFooter } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-footer';
import { useListings } from '@/features/listings';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { http } from '@/lib/api/http';
import Link from 'next/link';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const BG = '#090807';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';
const FAINT = '#6e6658';

const LISTING_STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, { label: string; color: string; bg: string }> = {
  draft:     { label: 'Draft',       color: '#9a8f7c', bg: 'rgba(110,102,88,0.15)' },
  published: { label: 'Zverejnená',  color: '#5fd17a', bg: 'rgba(46,160,67,0.15)' },
  reserved:  { label: 'Rezervovaná', color: '#e8d48b', bg: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.15)' },
  completed: { label: 'Dokončená',   color: '#8fa8d0', bg: 'rgba(80,120,180,0.15)' },
  archived:  { label: 'Archivovaná', color: '#a8a8a8', bg: 'rgba(80,80,80,0.20)' },
};

const COMPANY_STATUS_WARN: Record<string, string> = {
  draft: 'Firma je v koncepte. Požiadajte o overenie, aby ste mohli publikovať ponuky.',
  verification_submitted: 'Firma čaká na overenie administrátorom. Publikovanie bude dostupné po schválení.',
  rejected: 'Firma bola zamietnutá. Skontrolujte údaje a pošlite novú žiadosť o overenie.',
  suspended: 'Firma je pozastavená. Kontaktujte administrátora platformy.',
  inactive: 'Firma je neaktívna. Publikovanie nie je možné.',
};

type Listing = {
  id: string;
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  material_type?: string;
  materialType?: string;
  location?: string;
  quantity?: number | string;
  unit?: string;
  status?: string;
  created_at?: string;
  createdAt?: string;
};

export default function MyListingsPage() {
  const { data, isLoading } = useListings();
  const { data: companiesData } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['companies', 'my'],
    queryFn: () => http.get<any>('/companies'),
  });

  const raw: any = data;
  const items: Listing[] = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw?.data ?? raw?.items ?? [];

  // Prvá firma patriaca používateľovi
  const myCompany = (() => {
    const c = (companiesData as any)?.items ?? (companiesData as any)?.data ?? companiesData;
    if (Array.isArray(c) && c.length > 0) return c[0];
    return null;
  })();

  const companyStatus: string = myCompany?.status || 'draft';
  const isVerified = companyStatus === 'verified';
  const warningMessage = COMPANY_STATUS_WARN[companyStatus];

  return (
    <>
      <link
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,500&family=Outfit:wght@400;500;600&display=swap"
        rel="stylesheet"
      />
      <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG, fontFamily: 'Outfit, sans-serif' }}>
        <DashboardHeader />

        <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-10">
          {!isVerified && warningMessage && (
            <div
              className="mb-8 rounded-2xl border p-5"
              style={{
                borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 40%, transparent)',
                background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.06)',
              }}
            >
              <p className="mb-1 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
                Stav firmy: {companyStatus}
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed" style={{ color: FG }}>
                ⚠️ {warningMessage}
              </p>
              {companyStatus === 'draft' && (
                <Link
                  href="/dashboard/companies/new"
                  className="mt-3 inline-block text-sm underline underline-offset-4"
                  style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}
                >
                  Požiadať o overenie firmy →
                </Link>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mb-10 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
              <p className="mb-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
                MOJE PONUKY
              </p>
              <h1 className="text-4xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em] md:text-5xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
                Ponuky
              </h1>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/dashboard/listings/new"
              className="rounded-md px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold"
              style={{ background: GOLD, color: BG }}
            >
              + Pridať ponuku
            </Link>
          </div>

          {isLoading ? (
            <p style={{ color: MUTED }}>Načítavam…</p>
          ) : items.length === 0 ? (
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border py-16 text-center"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)' }}
            >
              <h3 className="text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
                Zatiaľ žiadne ponuky
              </h3>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                Pridaj svoju prvú ponuku a oslov zákazníkov.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {items.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.id}>
                  <Link
                    href={`/dashboard/listings/${item.id}`}
                    className="block rounded-2xl border p-5 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5"
                    style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)' }}
                  >
                    <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
                      {item.material_type || item.materialType || '—'}
                    </p>
                    <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>
                      {item.title || 'Ponuka'}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                      {item.description || '—'}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs" style={{ color: FAINT }}>
                      <span>{item.location || '—'}</span>
                      <span>
                        {item.quantity || '—'} {item.unit || ''}
                      </span>
                      <span
                        className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em]"
                        style={{
                          background: LISTING_STATUS_LABEL[item.status || 'draft']?.bg || 'rgba(110,102,88,0.15)',
                          color: LISTING_STATUS_LABEL[item.status || 'draft']?.color || '#9a8f7c',
                        }}
                      >
                        {LISTING_STATUS_LABEL[item.status || 'draft']?.label || item.status}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </main>

      <DashboardFooter />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

'use client';

import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { DashboardFooter } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-footer';
import { useListings } from '@/features/listings';
import Link from 'next/link';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const BG = '#090807';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';
const FAINT = '#6e6658';

type Listing = {
  id: string;
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  material_type?: string;
  materialType?: string;
  location?: string;
  quantity?: number | string;
  unit?: string;
  status?: string;
  created_at?: string;
  createdAt?: string;
};

export default function MyListingsPage() {
  const { data, isLoading } = useListings();

  const items: Listing[] = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw?.data ?? raw?.items ?? [];

              + Pridať ponuku
            </Link>
          </div>

            <p style={{ color: MUTED }}>Načítavam…</p>
          ) : items.length === 0 ? (
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border py-16 text-center"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)' }}
            >
              <h3 className="text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
                Zatiaľ žiadne ponuky
              </h3>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                Pridaj svoju prvú ponuku a oslov zákazníkov.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {items.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.id}>
                  <Link
                    href={`/dashboard/listings/${item.id}`}
                    className="block rounded-2xl border p-5 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5"
                    style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)' }}
                  >
                    <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
                      {item.material_type || item.materialType || '—'}
                    </p>
                    <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>
                      {item.title || 'Ponuka'}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                      {item.description || '—'}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs" style={{ color: FAINT }}>
                      <span>{item.location || '—'}</span>
                      <span>
                        {item.quantity || '—'} {item.unit || ''}
                      </span>
                      <span>{item.status || '—'}</span>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </main>

      <DashboardFooter />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useUnreadNotifications } from "@/features/notifications";
import { useMarketplace } from "@/features/listings";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { http } from "@/lib/api/http";
import { useT } from "@/lib/i18n/context";
import { DashboardHeader } from "@/components/layout/dashboard-header";
import { DashboardFooter } from "@/components/layout/dashboard-footer";
import { useLogout, useCurrentUser, useIsPlatformAdmin } from "@/features/auth";

type Listing = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  company: string;
  location: string;
  category: string;
  quantity: string;
  published: string;
  note: string;
  transport: string;
  images: string[];
};

const GOLD = "#c9a84c";
const GOLD_BRIGHT = "#e8d48b";
const BG = "#090807";
const FG = "#f3ead8";
const MUTED = "#9a8f7c";
const FAINT = "#6e6658";

const categoryKeys = ["all", "wood", "metal", "paper", "plastics", "bio"] as const;
const categorySkMap: Record<string, string> = {
  all: "Všetko", wood: "Drevo", metal: "Kov",
  paper: "Papier", plastics: "Plasty", bio: "Bioodpad",
};
const DASHBOARD_MATERIALS = [
  { key: 'drevo',    img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1542621334-a254cf47733d?w=800&q=80' },
  { key: 'kov',      img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1567095761054-7a02e69e5c43?w=800&q=80' },
  { key: 'papier',   img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1589994965851-a8f479c573a9?w=800&q=80' },
  { key: 'plasty',   img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1618363010061-a42f6e39e3d8?w=800&q=80' },
  { key: 'bioodpad', img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1611843467160-25afb8df1074?w=800&q=80' },
] as const;

const topLinkKeys = ["overview", "listings", "marketplace", "matches", "companies", "documents", "chat"] as const;
const PAGE_SIZE = 6;

function fixImageUrl(u: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!u) return null;
  let s = String(u).trim();
  s = s.replace(/\/api\/v1\/api\/v1\//g, "/api/v1/");
  if (/^https?:\/\//.test(s)) {
    return s.replace(/\/api\/v1\/api\/v1\//g, "/api/v1/");
  }
  // Backend URL z env, nie hardcoded localhost
  const base = (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || "https://waste-platform.onrender.com/api/v1")
    .replace(/\/api\/v1\/?$/, "");
  if (s.startsWith("/api/v1/")) return base + s;
  if (s.startsWith("/")) return base + "/api/v1" + s;
  return null;
}

function makeFormatRelative(t: (k: string, fb?: string) => string) {
  return (iso: string | null | undefined): string => {
    if (!iso) return "\u2014";
    const d = new Date(iso);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return "\u2014";
    const diff = Date.now() - d.getTime();
    const day = 86400000;
    if (diff < day) return t("dashboard.dateToday");
    if (diff < 2 * day) return t("dashboard.dateYesterday");
    const days = Math.floor(diff / day);
    if (days < 7) return t("dashboard.dateDaysAgo").replace("{n}", String(days));
    return d.toLocaleDateString();
  };
}

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const { t } = useT();
  const { data: me } = useCurrentUser();
  const { data: unreadData } = useUnreadNotifications();
  const unreadChat = Number((unreadData as any)?.unread || 0);
  const isAdmin = useIsPlatformAdmin();
  const myEmail = String((me as any)?.email || (me as any)?.user?.email || '');
  const { mutateAsync: logout, isPending: logoutPending } = useLogout();
  const formatRelative = useMemo(() => makeFormatRelative(t), [t]);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<string>("all");
  const [location, setLocation] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedImage, setSelectedImage] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const { data: apiResp, isLoading, error } = useMarketplace();

  const { data: companiesResp } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["companies-for-dashboard"],
    queryFn: () => http.get<any>("/companies"),
  });

  const companiesMap = useMemo(() => {
    const raw: any = companiesResp;
    const items: any[] = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw?.data ?? raw?.items ?? [];
    const m: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const c of items) {
      const n = c.tradingName || c.trading_name || c.legalName || c.legal_name || "";
      if (c.id && n) m[String(c.id)] = n;
    }
    return m;
  }, [companiesResp]);

  const listings: Listing[] = useMemo(() => {
    const raw: any = apiResp;
    const items: any[] = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw?.data ?? raw?.items ?? [];
    return items.map((it: any) => ({
      id: String(it.id),
      title: it.title ?? t("dashboard.titleNoName"),
      company:
        it.company?.name ??
        it.companyName ??
        companiesMap[String(it.companyId ?? it.company_id ?? "")] ??
        t("dashboard.companyUnknown"),
      location: it.location ?? "\u2014",
      category: it.materialType ?? it.material_type ?? "\u2014",
      quantity: [it.quantity, it.unit].filter(Boolean).join(" ") || "\u2014",
      published: formatRelative(it.createdAt ?? it.created_at),
      note: it.description ?? "",
      transport: it.transport ?? "\u2014",
      images: [fixImageUrl(it.imageUrl ?? it.image_url)].filter(Boolean) as string[],
    }));
  }, [apiResp, companiesMap, t, formatRelative]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return listings.filter((item) => {
      const matchesQuery =
        query.trim() === "" ||
        `${item.title} ${item.company} ${item.location} ${item.category} ${item.note} ${item.transport}`
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query.toLowerCase());
      const selectedSk = categorySkMap[category] || "Všetko";
      const matchesCategory = category === "all" || item.category === selectedSk;
      const matchesLocation =
        location.trim() === "" ||
        item.location.toLowerCase().includes(location.toLowerCase());
      return matchesQuery && matchesCategory && matchesLocation;
    });
  }, [query, category, location, listings]);

  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE));
  const currentPageItems = useMemo(() => {
    const start = (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
    return filtered.slice(start, start + PAGE_SIZE);
  }, [filtered, page]);

  function changeCategory(next: string) {
    setCategory(next);
    setPage(1);
  }
  function changePage(next: number) {
    if (next < 1 || next > pageCount) return;
    setPage(next);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  }

  const navHrefs: Record<string, string> = {
    listings: "/dashboard/listings",
    documents: "/dashboard/documents",
    companies: "/dashboard/companies",
    chat: "/dashboard/chat",
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG, fontFamily: "Outfit, sans-serif" }}>
      <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
      <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossOrigin="anonymous" />
      <link
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,400;1,9..144,500&family=Outfit:wght@400;500;600&display=swap"
        rel="stylesheet"
      />

      {/* HEADER */}
      <DashboardHeader />

      <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-10">
        {/* UVÍTANIE */}
        <section className="mb-14">
          <div className="rounded-3xl border p-6 md:p-10" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(201,168,76,0.08) 0%, rgba(9,8,7,0) 60%)' }}>
            <p className="mb-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
              {t("dashboard.welcome.eyebrow")}
            </p>
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-4xl leading-[1.05] tracking-[-0.02em] md:text-5xl" style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT, fontFamily: "Fraunces, serif" }}>
              {t("dashboard.welcome.title")}
            </h2>
            <p className="max-w-2xl text-base leading-relaxed md:text-lg" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
              {t("dashboard.welcome.desc")}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* MOTTO + HERO */}
        <section className="mb-12">
          <p className="mb-4 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
            {t("dashboard.motto.eyebrow")}
          </p>
          <h1 className="text-5xl leading-[1.05] tracking-[-0.02em] md:text-7xl" style={{ fontFamily: "Fraunces, serif" }}>
            {t("dashboard.motto.headline")}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-6 max-w-2xl text-base leading-relaxed md:text-lg" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
            {t("dashboard.motto.subline")}
          </p>
        </section>

        {/* FILTER — SEARCH */}
        <section className="mb-8">
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-[1.4fr_0.7fr_auto]">
            <input
              type="search"
              placeholder={t("dashboard.searchPlaceholder")}
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => { setQuery(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
              className="h-14 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-5 text-base outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }}
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder={t("dashboard.locationPlaceholder")}
              value={location}
              onChange={(e) => { setLocation(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
              className="h-14 rounded-md border bg-transparent px-5 text-base outline-none"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }}
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              className="h-14 rounded-md px-8 text-base font-semibold"
              style={{ background: GOLD, color: BG }}
            >
              {t("dashboard.searchBtn")}
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-base">
            <section className="grid gap-3">
              <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{t("dashboard.actions")}</p>
              <Link href="/dashboard/companies/new" className="rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm" style={{ borderColor: GOLD, color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>{t("dashboard.addCompany")}</Link>
              <Link href="/dashboard/listings/new" className="rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm" style={{ borderColor: GOLD, color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>{t("dashboard.addListing")}</Link>
              <Link href="/dashboard/documents/new" className="rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm" style={{ borderColor: GOLD, color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>{t("dashboard.addDocument")}</Link>
            </section>
          </div>

        {/* DLAŽDICE MATERIÁLOV */}
        <section className="mb-10">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-5">
            {DASHBOARD_MATERIALS.map((m) => {
              const active = category === m.key;
              return (
                <button
                  key={m.key}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => changeCategory(active ? 'all' : m.key)}
                  className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border text-left transition-colors"
                  style={{
                    borderColor: active ? GOLD : 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)',
                    aspectRatio: '4/5',
                  }}
                >
                  <img
                    src={m.img}
                    alt={t(`dashboard.materials.${m.key}.label`)}
                    className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover transition-opacity"
                    style={{ opacity: active ? 0.85 : 0.5 }}
                  />
                  <div
                    className="absolute inset-0"
                    style={{
                      background:
                        'linear-gradient(to top, rgba(9,8,7,0.95) 0%, rgba(9,8,7,0.5) 50%, transparent 100%)',
                    }}
                  />
                  <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-3 md:p-4">
                    <p className="ff text-2xl md:text-3xl" style={{ color: active ? GOLD : GOLD_BRIGHT }}>
                      {t(`dashboard.materials.${m.key}.label`)}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-snug md:text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                      {t(`dashboard.materials.${m.key}.desc`)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          {category !== 'all' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => changeCategory('all')}
              className="mt-3 text-sm underline underline-offset-4"
              style={{ color: MUTED }}
            >
              {t("dashboard.clearFilter")} · {categorySkMap[category] || category}
            </button>
          )}
        </section>

        {/* LIST HEAD */}
        <section className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t pt-8" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 15%, transparent)' }}>
          <div style={{ color: MUTED }}>
            {isLoading ? (
              <span>{t("common.loading")}</span>
            ) : error ? (
              <span style={{ color: '#c45c4a' }}>
                {t("dashboard.loadError")} {(error as any)?.message || t("dashboard.loadErrorUnknown")}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <>
                <strong style={{ color: FG }}>{filtered.length}</strong> {t("dashboard.activeListings")}
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* LISTING GRID */}
        <section>
          {currentPageItems.length === 0 && !isLoading ? (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border py-16 text-center" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)' }}>
              <h3 className="text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>{t("dashboard.notFound")}</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>{t("dashboard.notFoundHint")}</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {currentPageItems.map((item) => {
                const isOpen = openId === item.id;
                const heroImage = selectedImage[item.id] || item.images[0];
                return (
                  <article
                    key={item.id}
                    className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border transition-colors"
                    style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)' }}
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setOpenId(isOpen ? null : item.id)}
                      className="w-full text-left"
                    >
                      {/* image */}
                      {heroImage ? (
                        <div className="relative aspect-[16/10] w-full">
                          <img
                            src={heroImage}
                            alt={item.title}
                            className="h-full w-full object-cover opacity-90"
                            onError={(e) => {
                              (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none';
                            }}
                          />
                          <div className="absolute inset-0" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(to top, rgba(9,8,7,0.95) 0%, rgba(9,8,7,0.3) 40%, transparent 75%)' }} />
                          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-5">
                            <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{heroImage ? item.category : "Fotka čoskoro · " + item.category}</p>
                            <h3 className="mt-1 text-2xl leading-tight" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>{item.title}</h3>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <div className="relative min-h-[88px] w-full" style={{ background: '#12100e' }}>
                          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-5">
                            <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{item.category}</p>
                            <h3 className="mt-1 text-2xl leading-tight" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>{item.title}</h3>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {/* summary */}
                      <div className="p-5">
                        <p className="line-clamp-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>{item.note || "\u2014"}</p>
                        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs" style={{ color: FAINT }}>
                          <span>{item.company}</span>
                          <span>· {item.location}</span>
                          <span>· {item.quantity}</span>
                          <span>· {item.published}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </button>

                    {/* expanded detail */}
                    {isOpen && (
                      <div className="border-t p-5" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 15%, transparent)' }}>
                        <dl className="grid gap-3 text-sm">
                          {[
                            [t("dashboard.company"), item.company],
                            [t("dashboard.location"), item.location],
                            [t("dashboard.category"), item.category],
                            [t("dashboard.quantity"), item.quantity],
                            [t("dashboard.transport"), item.transport],
                            [t("dashboard.published"), item.published],
                          ].map(([k, v]) => (
                            <div key={String(k)} className="grid grid-cols-[130px_1fr] gap-3 border-b pb-2" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 10%, transparent)' }}>
                              <dt style={{ color: FAINT }}>{k}</dt>
                              <dd style={{ color: FG, fontWeight: 500 }}>{v}</dd>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </dl>

                        {/* thumbs */}
                        {item.images.length > 1 && (
                          <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
                            {item.images.map((image) => (
                              <button
                                key={image}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setSelectedImage((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: image }))}
                                className="rounded-md border p-0"
                                style={{ borderColor: heroImage === image ? GOLD : 'transparent' }}
                              >
                                <img src={image} alt="" className="h-16 w-16 rounded-md object-cover" />
                              </button>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        )}

                        <Link
                          href={`/dashboard/listings/${item.id}`}
                          className="mt-5 inline-block rounded-md px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold"
                          style={{ background: GOLD, color: BG }}
                        >
                          {t("dashboard.contactCompany")}
                        </Link>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </article>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        {/* PAGINATION */}
        {pageCount > 1 && (
          <nav className="mt-10 flex items-center justify-between border-t pt-6" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 15%, transparent)' }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => changePage(page - 1)}
              disabled={page === 1}
              className="rounded-md border px-4 py-2 text-sm disabled:opacity-40"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }}
            >
              {t("dashboard.prev")}
            </button>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {Array.from({ length: pageCount }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((num) => (
                <button
                  key={num}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => changePage(num)}
                  className="h-10 w-10 rounded-md border text-sm"
                  style={{
                    borderColor: num === page ? GOLD : 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)',
                    background: num === page ? GOLD : 'transparent',
                    color: num === page ? BG : FG,
                  }}
                >
                  {num}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => changePage(page + 1)}
              disabled={page === pageCount}
              className="rounded-md border px-4 py-2 text-sm disabled:opacity-40"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 30%, transparent)', color: FG }}
            >
              {t("dashboard.next")}
            </button>
          </nav>
        )}
      </main>

      <DashboardFooter />
    </div>
  );
}

function MyListingsMenu({ label }: { label: string }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const { t } = useT();
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold"
        style={{ borderColor: GOLD, color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}
        aria-expanded={open}
      >
        {label}
        <span className="text-xs" style={{ opacity: 0.7 }}>{open ? "▲" : "▼"}</span>
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div
          className="absolute right-0 top-full z-50 mt-2 min-w-[240px] rounded-xl border p-2"
          style={{ background: '#12100e', borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 35%, transparent)', boxShadow: '0 12px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)' }}
        >
          {[
            { href: "/dashboard/listings", ico: "👁", label: t("dashboard.viewAll") },
            { href: "/dashboard/listings/new", ico: "＋", label: t("dashboard.addNewListing") },
            { href: "/dashboard/listings?filter=draft", ico: "✎", label: t("dashboard.drafts") },
            { href: "/dashboard/listings?filter=published", ico: "✓", label: t("dashboard.publishedOnly") },
          ].map((it) => (
            <Link
              key={it.href}
              href={it.href}
              onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors hover:bg-white/5"
              style={{ color: FG }}
            >
              <span className="w-5 text-center" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{it.ico}</span>
              {it.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { config } from '@/lib/config';
import { getListingRecommendations } from '@/features/recommendations/api';

type ListingRow = { id: string; title: string; materialType?: string; location?: string };

async function fetchListings(): Promise<ListingRow[]> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/listings', {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
  });
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Listings request failed');
  const rows = (body.items || body.data || body) as ListingRow[];
  return Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
}

export default function AdminRecommendationsPage() {
  const listingsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: ['admin-listings'], queryFn: fetchListings });
  const firstId = listingsQuery.data?.[0]?.id;
  const recQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['admin-rec', firstId],
    queryFn: () => getListingRecommendations(firstId as string),
    enabled: !!firstId,
  });

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl bg-[#070707] p-6 text-[#f3e6c4]">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Admin</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-3xl">Odporucania</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Prehlad match-ov. Ziadny auto-deal.</p>
      {listingsQuery.error && <p className="mt-4 text-red-300">{(listingsQuery.error as Error).message}</p>}
      <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
        {(listingsQuery.data || []).map((row) => (
          <li key={row.id} className="rounded-xl border border-[#d4af37]/25 p-3">
            <a href={'/dashboard/listings/' + row.id} className="text-[#d4af37]">{row.title || row.id}</a>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#9a8b63]">{row.materialType} · {row.location}</p>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {recQuery.data && (
        <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 p-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Prva ponuka: {recQuery.data.items.length} kandidatov</p>
        </section>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
