================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/companies/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { config } from '@/lib/config';
import { useT } from '@/lib/i18n/context';

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

type Company = {
  id: string;
  legalName?: string;
  city?: string;
  status?: string;
  acceptedMaterialTypes?: string | null;
};

function cleanFilter(raw: string): string {
  return raw.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9áäčďéíľĺňóôŕšťúýžÁÄČĎÉÍĽĹŇÓÔŔŠŤÚÝŽ,.\-\s]/g, '').slice(0, 64);
}

async function fetchCompanies(page: number): Promise<{ items: Company[]; total: number }> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/companies?page=' + page + '&pageSize=' + PAGE_SIZE, {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
  });
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(typeof body.detail === 'string' ? body.detail : 'HTTP ' + res.status);
  const items = (body.items || body.data || []) as Company[];
  const total = Number(body.meta?.total ?? body.total ?? items.length);
  return { items: Array.isArray(items) ? items : [], total };
}

export default function CompaniesPage() {
  const { t } = useT();
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [material, setMaterial] = useState('');
  const { data, error, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['companies', page],
    queryFn: () => fetchCompanies(page),
  });
  const filter = cleanFilter(material).trim().toLowerCase();
  const items = (data?.items || []).filter((row) =>
    filter ? (row.acceptedMaterialTypes || '').toLowerCase().includes(filter) : true,
  );
  const total = data?.total || 0;
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE));

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#090807] text-[#f3ead8]">
      <DashboardHeader />
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl p-6">
        <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#c9a84c]">{t('companies.kicker')}</p>
        <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-3xl">{t('companies.title')}</h1>
        <Link href="/dashboard/companies/new" className="mt-3 inline-block text-sm text-[#c9a84c]">
          {t('companies.add')}
        </Link>
        <input
          value={material}
          onChange={(e) => setMaterial(cleanFilter(e.target.value))}
          placeholder={t('companies.filter')}
          maxLength={64}
          className="mt-4 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#c9a84c]/25 bg-black p-2 text-sm"
        />
        {isLoading && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#9a8f7c]">{t('companies.loading')}</p>}
        {error && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-red-300">{(error as Error).message}</p>}
        <ul className="mt-6 space-y-3">
          {items.map((row) => (
            <li key={row.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-[#c9a84c]/25 p-4">
              <p className="font-semibold">{row.legalName || row.id}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-[#9a8f7c]">{row.city} · {row.status}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#9a8f7c]">
                {t('companies.materials')}: {row.acceptedMaterialTypes || t('companies.empty')}
              </p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
          <button type="button" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)} className="text-[#c9a84c] disabled:text-[#6e6658]">
            {t('companies.prev')}
          </button>
          <span>{page} / {pages}</span>
          <button type="button" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)} className="text-[#c9a84c] disabled:text-[#6e6658]">
            {t('companies.next')}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/listings/[id]/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { config } from '../../../../lib/config';
import { RecommendationsPanel } from '../../../../features/recommendations/components/recommendations-panel';
import { TransportPanel } from '../../../../features/recommendations/components/transport-panel';
import { FreightPanel } from '../../../../features/recommendations/components/freight-panel';
import { postListingViewed } from '../../../../features/recommendations/api';
import { ListingInquiryButton } from '../../../../features/inquiries/components/listing-inquiry-button';

type ListingData = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  materialType: string;
  quantity: number;
  unit: string;
  location: string;
  status: string;
  description?: string | null;
};

async function fetchListing(listingId: string): Promise<ListingData> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/listings/' + listingId, {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
  });
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) {
    const detail = typeof body.detail === 'string' ? body.detail : 'Listing request failed';
    throw new Error(detail);
  }
  return (body.data ?? body) as ListingData;
}

export default function ListingDetailPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const listingId = params?.id;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (listingId) {
      void postListingViewed(listingId);
    }
  }, [listingId]);
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState('');
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['listing', listingId],
    queryFn: () => fetchListing(listingId as string),
    enabled: !!listingId,
    retry: 1,
  });

  if (!listingId) return <p className="p-6 text-[#d4af37]">Chyba ID ponuky.</p>;

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl bg-[#070707] p-6 text-[#f3e6c4]">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Ponuka</p>
      <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-3xl">{data?.title || 'Ponuka'}</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs text-[#9a8b63]">{listingId}</p>
      {isLoading && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Nacitavam ponuku…</p>}
      {error && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-red-300">{(error as Error).message}</p>}
      {data && (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 bg-black p-5 text-sm">
          <p>Material: {data.materialType}</p>
          <p>Mnozstvo: {data.quantity} {data.unit}</p>
          <p>Lokalita: {data.location}</p>
          <p>Stav: {data.status}</p>
          {data.description ? <p className="mt-2 text-[#cbb98a]">{data.description}</p> : null}
          <ListingInquiryButton listingId={listingId} listingTitle={data.title} />
        </div>
      )}
      <RecommendationsPanel
        listingId={listingId}
        listingTitle={data?.title}
        listingLocation={data?.location}
        materialType={data?.materialType}
        onPickDestination={setDestination}
      />
      <TransportPanel origin={data?.location} destination={destination} />
      <FreightPanel near={destination || data?.location} />
    </main>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/app/dashboard/listings/page.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { DashboardHeader } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-header';
import { DashboardFooter } from '@/components/layout/dashboard-footer';
import { useListings } from '@/features/listings';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { http } from '@/lib/api/http';
import Link from 'next/link';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const BG = '#090807';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';
const FAINT = '#6e6658';

const LISTING_STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, { label: string; color: string; bg: string }> = {
  draft:     { label: 'Draft',       color: '#9a8f7c', bg: 'rgba(110,102,88,0.15)' },
  published: { label: 'Zverejnená',  color: '#5fd17a', bg: 'rgba(46,160,67,0.15)' },
  reserved:  { label: 'Rezervovaná', color: '#e8d48b', bg: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.15)' },
  completed: { label: 'Dokončená',   color: '#8fa8d0', bg: 'rgba(80,120,180,0.15)' },
  archived:  { label: 'Archivovaná', color: '#a8a8a8', bg: 'rgba(80,80,80,0.20)' },
};

const COMPANY_STATUS_WARN: Record<string, string> = {
  draft: 'Firma je v koncepte. Požiadajte o overenie, aby ste mohli publikovať ponuky.',
  verification_submitted: 'Firma čaká na overenie administrátorom. Publikovanie bude dostupné po schválení.',
  rejected: 'Firma bola zamietnutá. Skontrolujte údaje a pošlite novú žiadosť o overenie.',
  suspended: 'Firma je pozastavená. Kontaktujte administrátora platformy.',
  inactive: 'Firma je neaktívna. Publikovanie nie je možné.',
};

type Listing = {
  id: string;
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  material_type?: string;
  materialType?: string;
  location?: string;
  quantity?: number | string;
  unit?: string;
  status?: string;
  created_at?: string;
  createdAt?: string;
};

export default function MyListingsPage() {
  const { data, isLoading } = useListings();
  const { data: companiesData } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['companies', 'my'],
    queryFn: () => http.get<any>('/companies'),
  });

  const raw: any = data;
  const items: Listing[] = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw?.data ?? raw?.items ?? [];

  // Prvá firma patriaca používateľovi
  const myCompany = (() => {
    const c = (companiesData as any)?.items ?? (companiesData as any)?.data ?? companiesData;
    if (Array.isArray(c) && c.length > 0) return c[0];
    return null;
  })();

  const companyStatus: string = myCompany?.status || 'draft';
  const isVerified = companyStatus === 'verified';
  const warningMessage = COMPANY_STATUS_WARN[companyStatus];

  return (
    <>
      <link
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,500&family=Outfit:wght@400;500;600&display=swap"
        rel="stylesheet"
      />
      <div className="min-h-screen" style={{ background: BG, color: FG, fontFamily: 'Outfit, sans-serif' }}>
        <DashboardHeader />

        <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-10">
          {!isVerified && warningMessage && (
            <div
              className="mb-8 rounded-2xl border p-5"
              style={{
                borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 40%, transparent)',
                background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.06)',
              }}
            >
              <p className="mb-1 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
                Stav firmy: {companyStatus}
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed" style={{ color: FG }}>
                ⚠️ {warningMessage}
              </p>
              {companyStatus === 'draft' && (
                <Link
                  href="/dashboard/companies/new"
                  className="mt-3 inline-block text-sm underline underline-offset-4"
                  style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}
                >
                  Požiadať o overenie firmy →
                </Link>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mb-10 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
              <p className="mb-3 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
                MOJE PONUKY
              </p>
              <h1 className="text-4xl leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em] md:text-5xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
                Ponuky
              </h1>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/dashboard/listings/new"
              className="rounded-md px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold"
              style={{ background: GOLD, color: BG }}
            >
              + Pridať ponuku
            </Link>
          </div>

          {isLoading ? (
            <p style={{ color: MUTED }}>Načítavam…</p>
          ) : items.length === 0 ? (
            <div
              className="rounded-2xl border py-16 text-center"
              style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 20%, transparent)' }}
            >
              <h3 className="text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif' }}>
                Zatiaľ žiadne ponuky
              </h3>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                Pridaj svoju prvú ponuku a oslov zákazníkov.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {items.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.id}>
                  <Link
                    href={`/dashboard/listings/${item.id}`}
                    className="block rounded-2xl border p-5 transition-colors hover:bg-white/5"
                    style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)' }}
                  >
                    <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: GOLD }}>
                      {item.material_type || item.materialType || '—'}
                    </p>
                    <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl" style={{ fontFamily: 'Fraunces, serif', color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>
                      {item.title || 'Ponuka'}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                      {item.description || '—'}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs" style={{ color: FAINT }}>
                      <span>{item.location || '—'}</span>
                      <span>
                        {item.quantity || '—'} {item.unit || ''}
                      </span>
                      <span
                        className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em]"
                        style={{
                          background: LISTING_STATUS_LABEL[item.status || 'draft']?.bg || 'rgba(110,102,88,0.15)',
                          color: LISTING_STATUS_LABEL[item.status || 'draft']?.color || '#9a8f7c',
                        }}
                      >
                        {LISTING_STATUS_LABEL[item.status || 'draft']?.label || item.status}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </main>

      <DashboardFooter />
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/features/companies/api.ts
================================================================================
import { http } from '../../lib/api/http';
import { PaginatedResponse } from '../../lib/types/common';
import { Company } from './types';
import { CompanyInput } from './schemas';

export const companiesApi = {
  getCompanies: (params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<PaginatedResponse<Company>>('/companies', { ...params }),

  getCompany: (id: string) =>
    http.get<Company>(`/companies/${id}`),

  createCompany: (data: CompanyInput) =>
    http.post<Company>('/companies', data),

  updateCompany: (id: string, data: Partial<CompanyInput>) =>
    http.patch<Company>(`/companies/${id}`, data),

  transitionStatus: (id: string, toStatus: string) =>
    http.patch<Company>(`/companies/${id}/status`, { toStatus }),

  rpoCheck: (ico: string) =>
    http.get<{
      ok: boolean;
      exists: boolean;
      ico: string | null;
      legalName: string | null;
      city: string | null;
      reason: string;
    }>(`/companies/rpo-check?ico=${encodeURIComponent(ico)}`),
};

FILE: apps/web/src/features/companies/components/accepted-materials-cell.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { companiesApi } from '../api';
import { useT } from '@/lib/i18n/context';

export function AcceptedMaterialsCell({
  id,
  value,
}: {
  id: string;
  value?: string | null;
}) {
  const [text, setText] = useState(value || '');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const { t } = useT();

  async function save() {
    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      await companiesApi.updateCompany(id, {
        acceptedMaterialTypes: text.trim() || null,
      } as never);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Save failed');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-w-[160px]">
      <input
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value.slice(0, 120))}
        placeholder={t('admin.materialsPlaceholder')}
        className="w-full rounded border border-[#c9a84c]/30 bg-black px-2 py-1 text-xs text-[#f3ead8]"
      />
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={saving}
        onClick={() => void save()}
        className="mt-1 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-[#c9a84c]"
      >
        {saving ? '…' : t('admin.saveMaterials')}
      </button>
      {error && <p className="text-[10px] text-red-300">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/features/companies/components/companies-table.tsx

async function checkRpoBeforeVerify(ico?: string | null) {
  const raw = (ico || "").trim();
  if (!raw) return "IČO chýba. RPO sa neda overit.";
  try {
    const mod = await import("../api");
    const api = (mod as any).companiesApi || (mod as any).companyApi;
    const res = await api.rpoCheck(raw);
    if (!res.exists) {
      if (res.reason === "ico_invalid") return "IČO je neplatné (musi mat 8 cislic). RPO: neexistuje.";
      if (res.reason === "not_found") return "IČO " + (res.ico || raw) + " v RPO neexistuje.";
      return "RPO overenie zlyhalo (" + res.reason + ").";
    }
    return "RPO OK: " + (res.legalName || "") + (res.city ? " · " + res.city : "");
  } catch (e: any) {
    return "RPO sa nepodarilo overit.";
  }
}

'use client';

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useCompanies } from '../hooks/use-companies';
import { companiesApi } from '../api';
import { cn } from '../../../lib/utils/cn';
import { useIsPlatformAdmin } from '../../auth/hooks/use-is-platform-admin';
import { useT } from '@/lib/i18n/context';

const GOLD = '#c9a84c';
const GOLD_BRIGHT = '#e8d48b';
const FG = '#f3ead8';
const MUTED = '#9a8f7c';
const FAINT = '#6e6658';

const STATUS_STYLE: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; label: string }> = {
  draft:                  { bg: 'rgba(110,102,88,0.15)', text: '#9a8f7c', label: 'Draft' },
  verification_submitted: { bg: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.15)', text: '#e8d48b', label: 'Čaká na overenie' },
  verified:               { bg: 'rgba(46,160,67,0.15)',  text: '#5fd17a', label: 'Overená' },
  rejected:               { bg: 'rgba(196,92,74,0.15)',  text: '#e8a598', label: 'Zamietnutá' },
  suspended:              { bg: 'rgba(196,92,74,0.20)',  text: '#f0b8a8', label: 'Pozastavená' },
  inactive:               { bg: 'rgba(80,80,80,0.20)',   text: '#a8a8a8', label: 'Neaktívna' },
};

function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: string }) {
  const s = STATUS_STYLE[status] ?? STATUS_STYLE.draft;
  return (
    <span
      className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium"
      style={{ background: s.bg, color: s.text }}
    >
      {s.label}
    </span>
  );
}

function ActionButton({
  onClick,
  disabled,
  variant,
  children,
}: {
  onClick: () => void;
  disabled: boolean;
  variant: 'primary' | 'danger' | 'warn';
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const styles = {
    primary: { background: '#2ea043', color: '#fff' },
    danger: { background: '#c45c4a', color: '#fff' },
    warn: { background: GOLD, color: '#090807' },
  }[variant];

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      disabled={disabled}
      className="rounded px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-50 transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
      style={styles}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

import { AcceptedMaterialsCell } from './accepted-materials-cell';
export const CompaniesTable = () => {
  const { t } = useT();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useCompanies();
  const isAdmin = useIsPlatformAdmin();

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, toStatus, ico }: { id: string; toStatus: string; ico?: string | null }) =>
      (async () => {
        if (toStatus === 'verified') {
          const msg = await checkRpoBeforeVerify(ico);
          window.alert(msg);
        }
        return companiesApi.transitionStatus(id, toStatus);
      })(),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['companies'] }),
  });

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-4 text-center" style={{ color: MUTED }}>{t('tables.loading')}</div>;
  if (error) return <div className="p-4 text-center" style={{ color: '#e8a598' }}>{t('tables.error')}</div>;

  const companies = (data as any)?.items ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border" style={{ borderColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, #c9a84c 25%, transparent)' }}>
      {mutation.isError && (
        <div className="px-4 py-2 text-sm" style={{ background: 'rgba(196,92,74,0.12)', color: '#e8a598' }}>
          {(mutation.error as any)?.message || t('tables.adminOnly')}
        </div>
      )}

      <table className="min-w-full text-sm" style={{ color: FG }}>
        <thead>
          <tr style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(201,168,76,0.15)' }}>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-semibold" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{t('tables.companyName')}</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-semibold" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{t('tables.ico')}</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-semibold" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{t('tables.city')}</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-semibold" style={{ color: GOLD }}>Material</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-semibold" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{t('tables.status')}</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-semibold" style={{ color: GOLD }}>{t('tables.actions')}</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {companies.length === 0 ? (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={6} className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                {t('tables.empty')}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ) : (
            companies.map((company: any) => {
              const status = company.status || 'draft';
              return (
                <tr key={company.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(201,168,76,0.08)' }}>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium" style={{ color: FG }}>
                    {company.legalName || company.name}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: MUTED }}>
                    {company.registrationNumber || company.registration_number}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3" style={{ color: MUTED }}>{company.city}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3"><AcceptedMaterialsCell id={company.id} value={company.acceptedMaterialTypes || company.accepted_material_types} /></td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <StatusBadge status={status} />
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {/* OWNER akcie (pre všetkých, backend kontroluje) */}
                      {status === 'draft' && (
                        <ActionButton
                          variant="warn"
                          disabled={mutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: company.id, toStatus: 'verification_submitted' })}
                        >
                          {t('tables.requestVerify')}
                        </ActionButton>
                      )}

                      {/* ADMIN akcie */}
                      {isAdmin && status === 'verification_submitted' && (
                        <>
                          <ActionButton
                            variant="primary"
                            disabled={mutation.isPending}
                            onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: company.id, toStatus: 'verified', ico: company.taxIdentifier || company.tax_identifier })}
                          >
                            Overiť
                          </ActionButton>
                          <ActionButton
                            variant="danger"
                            disabled={mutation.isPending}
                            onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: company.id, toStatus: 'rejected' })}
                          >
                            Zamietnuť
                          </ActionButton>
                        </>
                      )}

                      {isAdmin && status === 'verified' && (
                        <ActionButton
                          variant="danger"
                          disabled={mutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: company.id, toStatus: 'suspended' })}
                        >
                          Pozastaviť
                        </ActionButton>
                      )}

                      {isAdmin && status === 'suspended' && (
                        <ActionButton
                          variant="primary"
                          disabled={mutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: company.id, toStatus: 'verified', ico: company.taxIdentifier || company.tax_identifier })}
                        >
                          Reaktivovať
                        </ActionButton>
                      )}

                      {isAdmin && status === 'rejected' && (
                        <ActionButton
                          variant="warn"
                          disabled={mutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: company.id, toStatus: 'draft' })}
                        >
                          Obnoviť do draft
                        </ActionButton>
                      )}

                      {isAdmin && status === 'inactive' && (
                        <ActionButton
                          variant="primary"
                          disabled={mutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: company.id, toStatus: 'verified', ico: company.taxIdentifier || company.tax_identifier })}
                        >
                          Aktivovať
                        </ActionButton>
                      )}

                      {!isAdmin && status === 'verification_submitted' && (
                        <span className="text-xs" style={{ color: GOLD_BRIGHT }}>
                          {t('tables.awaitingAdmin')}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })
          )}
        </tbody>
      </table>

      {isAdmin && (
        <div className="border-t px-4 py-2 text-xs" style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.15)', color: FAINT }}>
          {t('tables.adminMode')}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

'use client';

import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { companySchema, CompanyInput } from '../schemas';
import { useCreateCompany } from '../hooks/use-create-company';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '../../../components/ui/input';
import { FormField } from '../../../components/ui/form-field';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useT } from "@/lib/i18n/context";

export const CompanyForm = () => {
  const { mutate: createCompany, isPending, error } = useCreateCompany();
  const router = useRouter();

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<CompanyInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(companySchema),
    defaultValues: {
      countryCode: 'SK',
      companyType: 'seller_buyer',
      sellerCapability: true,
      buyerCapability: true,
      logisticsCapability: false,
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = (data: CompanyInput) => {
    const payload = {
      ...data,
      email: data.email || null,
      tradingName: data.tradingName || null,
      taxIdentifier: data.taxIdentifier || null,
      vatIdentifier: data.vatIdentifier || null,
      phone: data.phone || null,
    };
    createCompany(payload as any, {
      onSuccess: () => router.push('/dashboard'),
    });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-4 w-full max-w-2xl p-6 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-md">
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6 text-gray-900">Registrovať novú firmu</h2>

      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-md border border-red-200">
          {(error as any).message || 'Chyba pri registrácii firmy'}
        </div>
      )}

      <FormField label="Obchodný názov (legal name)" error={errors.legalName?.message}>
        <Input {...register('legalName')} disabled={isPending} placeholder="Firma s.r.o." />
      </FormField>

      <FormField label="Obchodné meno (voliteľné)" error={errors.tradingName?.message}>
        <Input {...register('tradingName')} disabled={isPending} />
      </FormField>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <FormField label="Registracne cislo" error={errors.registrationNumber?.message}>
          <Input {...register('registrationNumber')} disabled={isPending} />
        </FormField>
        <FormField label="IČO" error={errors.taxIdentifier?.message}>
          <Input {...register('taxIdentifier')} disabled={isPending} placeholder="8 cislic" />
        </FormField>
        <FormField label="DIČ" error={errors.vatIdentifier?.message}>
          <Input {...register('vatIdentifier')} disabled={isPending} />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <FormField label="Typ firmy (rola)" error={errors.companyType?.message}>
          <select
            {...register('companyType')}
            disabled={isPending}
            className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-white"
          >
            <option value="seller_buyer">Predajca + Kupujúci</option>
            <option value="seller">Predajca</option>
            <option value="buyer">Kupujúci</option>
            <option value="logistics_partner">Logistika</option>
          </select>
        </FormField>
        <FormField label="Kód krajiny" error={errors.countryCode?.message}>
          <Input {...register('countryCode')} disabled={isPending} placeholder="SK" />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      <FormField label="Adresa" error={errors.addressLine1?.message}>
        <Input {...register('addressLine1')} disabled={isPending} />
      </FormField>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <FormField label="Mesto" error={errors.city?.message}>
          <Input {...register('city')} disabled={isPending} />
        </FormField>
        <FormField label="PSČ" error={errors.postalCode?.message}>
          <Input {...register('postalCode')} disabled={isPending} />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <FormField label="E-mail firmy" error={errors.email?.message}>
          <Input type="email" {...register('email')} disabled={isPending} />
        </FormField>
        <FormField label="Telefón" error={errors.phone?.message}>
          <Input {...register('phone')} disabled={isPending} />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-end gap-4 mt-6">
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.back()}>
          Zrušiť
        </Button>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={isPending}>
          {isPending ? 'Ukladám...' : 'Registrovať'}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
};

FILE: apps/web/src/features/companies/schemas.ts

import { z } from 'zod';

export const companySchema = z.object({
  legalName: z.string().min(2, 'Názov firmy musí mať aspoň 2 znaky'),
  registrationNumber: z.string().min(5, 'IČO musí mať aspoň 5 znakov'),
  companyType: z.enum(['seller', 'buyer', 'seller_buyer', 'logistics_partner'], {
    errorMap: () => ({ message: 'Vyber typ firmy' }),
  }),
  countryCode: z.string().min(2).default('SK'),
  addressLine1: z.string().min(5, 'Adresa je povinná'),
  city: z.string().min(2, 'Mesto je povinné'),
  postalCode: z.string().min(3, 'PSČ je povinné'),
  sellerCapability: z.boolean().default(true),
  buyerCapability: z.boolean().default(true),
  logisticsCapability: z.boolean().default(false),
  tradingName: z.string().optional(),
  taxIdentifier: z.string().regex(/^\\d{8}$/, 'ICO musi mat 8 cislic'),
  vatIdentifier: z.string().optional(),
  email: z.string().email('Neplatný e-mail').optional().or(z.literal('')),
  phone: z.string().optional(),
});

export type CompanyInput = z.infer<typeof companySchema>;

import { http } from '../../lib/api/http';

export type InquiryCreateInput = {
  listingId: string;
  companyId: string;
  message: string;
  contactEmail: string;
  contactPhone?: string;
};

export const inquiriesApi = {
  create: (data: InquiryCreateInput) =>
    http.post<{ data: any }>('/inquiries', data),

  getReceived: (params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<{ items: any[]; meta: any }>('/inquiries/received', { ...params }),

  getSent: (params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<{ items: any[]; meta: any }>('/inquiries/sent', { ...params }),

  updateStatus: (id: string, status: 'open' | 'answered' | 'closed') =>
    http.patch<{ data: any }>(`/inquiries/${id}/status`, { status }),
};

FILE: apps/web/src/features/inquiries/components/inquiries-table.tsx

'use client';

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useReceivedInquiries, useSentInquiries } from '../hooks/use-inquiries';
import { inquiriesApi } from '../api';

type Props = { mode: 'received' | 'sent' };

const statusClass = (status: string) => {
  if (status === 'answered') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-800';
  if (status === 'closed') return 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-700';
  return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800';
};

export const InquiriesTable = ({ mode }: Props) => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const received = useReceivedInquiries();
  const sent = useSentInquiries();
  const q = mode === 'received' ? received : sent;

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: 'answered' | 'closed' }) =>
      inquiriesApi.updateStatus(id, status),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['inquiries'] });
    },
  });

  if (q.isLoading) return <div className="p-4 text-center">Načítavam dopyty...</div>;
  if (q.error) return <div className="p-4 text-red-500 text-center">Chyba pri načítaní dopytov.</div>;

  const items = (q.data as any)?.items ?? [];
  const colSpan = mode === 'received' ? 6 : 5;

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
      {mutation.isError && (
        <div className="bg-red-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-red-700">Nepodarilo sa zmeniť status.</div>
      )}
      <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-200 bg-white text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-gray-50">
          <tr>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Ponuka</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Správa</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">E-mail</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Telefón</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Status</th>
            {mode === 'received' && (
              <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Akcie</th>
            )}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
          {items.length === 0 ? (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={colSpan} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-gray-500">
                {mode === 'received' ? 'Žiadne prijaté dopyty.' : 'Žiadne odoslané dopyty.'}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ) : (
            items.map((inq: any) => (
              <tr key={inq.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                <td className="px-4 py-2 font-medium text-gray-900">
                  {inq.listingTitle || '—'}
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-900 max-w-xs truncate">{inq.message}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{inq.contactEmail}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{inq.contactPhone || '—'}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2">
                  <span
                    className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${statusClass(
                      inq.status
                    )}`}
                  >
                    {inq.status}
                  </span>
                </td>
                {mode === 'received' && (
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 space-x-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                    {inq.status !== 'answered' && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={mutation.isPending}
                        onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: inq.id, status: 'answered' })}
                        className="rounded bg-green-600 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-white hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        Odpovedané
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {inq.status !== 'closed' && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={mutation.isPending}
                        onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ id: inq.id, status: 'closed' })}
                        className="rounded bg-gray-600 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-white hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        Uzavrieť
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </td>
                )}
              </tr>
            ))
          )}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/features/inquiries/components/listing-inquiry-button.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { inquiriesApi } from '../api';
import { useCurrentUser } from '@/features/auth/hooks/use-current-user';

export function ListingInquiryButton({
  listingId,
  listingTitle,
}: {
  listingId: string;
  listingTitle?: string;
}) {
  const me = useCurrentUser();
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const companyId = me.data?.memberships?.[0]?.companyId;
  const email = me.data?.email || '';

  const send = async () => {
    if (!companyId) {
      setMsg('Najprv si zaloz a over firmu.');
      return;
    }
    setPending(true);
    setMsg(null);
    try {
      await inquiriesApi.create({
        listingId,
        companyId,
        contactEmail: email,
        message: 'Dopyt k ponuke ' + (listingTitle || listingId) + '.',
      });
      setMsg('Dopyt odoslany.');
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const text =
        e instanceof Error
          ? e.message
          : typeof e === 'object' && e && 'message' in e
            ? String((e as { message: unknown }).message)
            : 'Dopyt zlyhal';
      setMsg(text);
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="mt-4">
      <button
        type="button"
        disabled={pending}
        onClick={() => void send()}
        className="rounded-full border border-[#d4af37]/50 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-[#d4af37]"
      >
        {pending ? 'Posielam dopyt…' : 'Poslat dopyt'}
      </button>
      {msg ? <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">{msg}</p> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useReceivedInquiries } from '../hooks/use-inquiries';

  if (openCount <= 0) return null;
  return (
    <span className="ml-2 inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-red-600 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
      {openCount} nový
    </span>
  );
};

FILE: apps/web/src/features/listings/api.ts
================================================================================
import { http } from '../../lib/api/http';
import { PaginatedResponse } from '../../lib/types/common';
import { Listing } from './types';
import { ListingInput } from './schemas';

export const listingsApi = {
  getListings: (params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<PaginatedResponse<Listing>>('/listings', { ...params }),

  getMarketplace: (params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<PaginatedResponse<Listing>>('/listings/marketplace', { ...params }),

  getListing: (id: string) =>
    http.get<Listing>(`/listings/${id}`),

  createListing: (data: ListingInput) =>
    http.post<Listing>('/listings', data),

  // Backend vystavuje PATCH /listings/{id} (nie PUT). PUT vracalo 405.
  updateListing: (id: string, data: Partial<ListingInput> & { status?: string }) =>
    http.patch<Listing>(`/listings/${id}`, data),

  publishListing: (id: string) =>
    http.post<{ data: any }>(`/listings/${id}/publish`, {}),

  deleteListing: (id: string) =>
    http.delete(`/listings/${id}`),

  recordView: (id: string) =>
    http.post<{ ok: boolean }>(`/listings/${id}/view`, {}),

  getStats: (id: string) =>
    http.get<{ views: number; uniqueViewers: number }>(`/listings/${id}/stats`),

  getMatches: (materialType: string, params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<PaginatedResponse<Listing>>(`/listings/matches?materialType=${encodeURIComponent(materialType)}`, { ...params }),
};

'use client';

import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { listingSchema, ListingInput } from '../schemas';
import { useCreateListing } from '../hooks/use-create-listing';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '../../../components/ui/input';
import { FormField } from '../../../components/ui/form-field';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { companiesApi } from '../../companies/api';
import { uploadFile } from '../../uploads/api';
import { config } from '../../../lib/config';
import { useT } from "@/lib/i18n/context";

export const ListingForm = () => {
  const { t } = useT();
  const { mutate: createListing, isPending, error } = useCreateListing();
  const router = useRouter();

  const { data: companiesData, isLoading: isLoadingCompanies } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['companies'],
    queryFn: () => companiesApi.getCompanies(),
  });

  const companies = (companiesData as any)?.items ?? [];
  const hasCompanies = companies.length > 0;

  const myCompany = companies[0];
  const companyStatus: string = myCompany?.status || 'draft';
  const isVerified = companyStatus === 'verified';

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    setValue,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<ListingInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(listingSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      imageUrl: '',
      quantity: 0,
      unit: 'kg',
      currency: 'EUR',
      condition: 'použité',
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = (data: ListingInput) => {
    if (!hasCompanies) {
      return;
    }

    createListing(data as any, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        router.push('/dashboard');
      },
    });
  };

  if (!isLoadingCompanies && !hasCompanies) {
    return (
      <div className="w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-950/95 p-6 shadow-xl shadow-black/30 sm:p-8">
        <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-300">
          Prvý krok
        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-white">
          Najprv pridajte firmu
        </h2>

        <p className="mt-2 max-w-lg text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-300">
          Každá ponuka musí patriť konkrétnej firme. Po vytvorení firmy sa sem môžete
          okamžite vrátiť a pridať ponuku materiálu.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <Button
            type="button"
            className="bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-500"
            onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard/companies/new')}
          >
            + Pridať firmu
          </Button>

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="border-slate-600 bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-800"
            onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}
          >
            Späť na dashboard
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
    {hasCompanies && !isVerified && (
      <div
        className="mb-4 w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl border p-5"
        style={{
          borderColor: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.4)',
          background: 'rgba(201,168,76,0.06)',
        }}
      >
        <p className="mb-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.25em]" style={{ color: '#c9a84c' }}>
          Stav firmy: {companyStatus}
        </p>
        <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed" style={{ color: '#f3ead8' }}>
          ⚠️ Vaša firma ešte nie je overená. Ponuku môžete vytvoriť, ale
          publikovať ju bude možné až po schválení administrátorom.
        </p>
      </div>
    )}

    <form
      onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
      className="w-full max-w-2xl space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-950/95 p-6 shadow-xl shadow-black/30 sm:p-8"
    >
      <div className="border-b border-slate-700/70 pb-4">
        <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-300">
          Nová ponuka
        </p>
        <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold text-white">{t('listingForm.titleAdd')}</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
          Vyplňte údaje o materiáli, ktorý chcete ponúknuť.
        </p>
      </div>

      {isLoadingCompanies && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-3 text-sm text-slate-300">
          Načítavam firmy…
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-900/70 bg-red-950/40 p-3 text-sm text-red-200">
          {(error as any).message || 'Chyba pri vytváraní ponuky'}
        </div>
      )}

      <FormField label="Firma" error={errors.companyId?.message}>
        <select
          {...register('companyId')}
          disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
          className="h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-600 bg-slate-900 px-3 text-sm text-slate-100 outline-none transition focus:border-emerald-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500/30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          <option value="">— vyberte firmu —</option>
          {companies.map((company: any) => (
            <option key={company.id} value={company.id}>
              {company.legalName || company.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </FormField>

      <FormField label={t('listingForm.nameLabel')} error={errors.title?.message}>
        <Input
          {...register('title')}
          disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
          placeholder="Zvyšky železa"
          className="rounded-xl"
        />
      </FormField>

      <FormField label={t('listingForm.materialLabel')} error={errors.materialType?.message}>
        <Input
          {...register('materialType')}
          disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
          placeholder="železo, plast, drevo..."
          className="rounded-xl"
        />
      </FormField>

      <FormField label="Popis" error={errors.description?.message}>
        <textarea
          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-600 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-500 outline-none transition focus:border-emerald-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500/30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          rows={3}
          {...register('description')}
          disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
        />
      </FormField>

      <FormField label="URL fotky (voliteľné)" error={(errors as any).imageUrl?.message}>
        <Input
          placeholder="https://... alebo nahrajte súbor nižšie"
          {...register('imageUrl')}
          disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
          className="rounded-xl"
        />

        <input
          type="file"
          accept="image/*,.pdf"
          className="mt-2 block w-full text-sm text-slate-300 file:mr-3 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:bg-slate-800 file:px-3 file:py-2 file:text-sm file:font-medium file:text-slate-100 hover:file:bg-slate-700"
          disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
          onChange={async (event) => {
            const file = event.target.files?.[0];
            if (!file) return;

            try {
              const response = await uploadFile(file);
              const base = config.apiBaseUrl;
              const url = response.url.startsWith('http')
                ? response.url
                : base + response.url;

              setValue('imageUrl', url as any, { shouldValidate: true });
            } catch (uploadError: any) {
              alert(uploadError?.message || 'Nahranie súboru zlyhalo');
            }
          }}
        />
      </FormField>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <FormField label="Množstvo" error={errors.quantity?.message}>
          <Input
            type="number"
            step="0.01"
            {...register('quantity', { valueAsNumber: true })}
            disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
            className="rounded-xl"
          />
        </FormField>

        <FormField label="Jednotka" error={errors.unit?.message}>
          <Input
            {...register('unit')}
            disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
            placeholder="kg"
            className="rounded-xl"
          />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <FormField label="Cena (voliteľné)" error={errors.price?.message}>
          <Input
            type="number"
            step="0.01"
            {...register('price', { valueAsNumber: true })}
            disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
            className="rounded-xl"
          />
        </FormField>

        <FormField label="Mena" error={errors.currency?.message}>
          <Input
            {...register('currency')}
            disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
            className="rounded-xl"
          />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <FormField label={t('listingForm.conditionLabel')} error={errors.condition?.message}>
          <Input
            {...register('condition')}
            disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
            placeholder="nové / použité / odpad"
            className="rounded-xl"
          />
        </FormField>

        <FormField label="Lokalita" error={errors.location?.message}>
          <Input
            {...register('location')}
            disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies}
            placeholder="Bratislava"
            className="rounded-xl"
          />
        </FormField>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-700/70 pt-5">
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          className="border-slate-600 bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-800"
          onClick={() => router.back()}
        >
          Zrušiť
        </Button>

        <Button
          type="submit"
          disabled={isPending || isLoadingCompanies || !hasCompanies}
          className="bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-500"
        >
          {isPending ? 'Ukladám...' : 'Vytvoriť ponuku'}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
    </>
  );
};

FILE: apps/web/src/features/listings/components/listings-table.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useListings } from '../hooks/use-listings';
import { useCompanies } from '../../companies/hooks/use-companies';
import { listingsApi } from '../api';
import { cn } from '../../../lib/utils/cn';
import { useT } from "@/lib/i18n/context";

export const ListingsTable = () => {
  const { t } = useT();
  const router = useRouter();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useListings();
  const { data: companiesData } = useCompanies();
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const companies = (companiesData as any)?.items ?? [];
  const verified =
    companies.length > 0 && companies.every((c: any) => c.status === 'verified')
      ? true
      : companies.some((c: any) => c.status === 'verified');

  // map companyId → status
  const companyStatusById: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const c of companies) {
    if (c.id) companyStatusById[String(c.id)] = c.status || 'draft';
  }

  const handlePublish = async (id: string, companyId?: string) => {
    setActionError(null);
    const st = companyId ? companyStatusById[String(companyId)] : undefined;
    const can =
      st === 'verified' ||
      (!companyId && verified);
    if (!can) {
      setActionError('Publikovať môže len overená firma (status verified). Najprv over firmu.');
      return;
    }
    setBusyId(id);
    try {
      await listingsApi.publishListing(id);
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['listings'] });
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['marketplace'] });
    } catch (e: any) {
      setActionError(e?.message || 'Publikácia zlyhala.');
    } finally {
      setBusyId(null);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-4 text-center">{t('tables.loading')}</div>;
  if (error) return <div className="p-4 text-red-500 text-center">{t('tables.error')}</div>;

  const listings = (data as any)?.items ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
      {actionError && (
        <div className="bg-red-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-red-600">{actionError}</div>
      )}
      {!verified && companies.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-amber-800">
          Firma ešte nie je overená — publikovanie je zablokované, kým nebude status{' '}
          <strong>verified</strong>.
        </div>
      )}
      <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-200 bg-white text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-gray-50">
          <tr>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Názov</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Množstvo</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Jednotka</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Akcie</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
          {listings.length === 0 ? (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-gray-500">
                {t('tables.empty')}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ) : (
            listings.map((listing: any) => {
              const st = companyStatusById[String(listing.companyId)] || '';
              const canPublish = st === 'verified' || (!st && verified);
              return (
                <tr
                  key={listing.id}
                  className="hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer"
                  onClick={() => router.push(`/dashboard/listings/${listing.id}`)}
                >
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 font-medium text-gray-900">{listing.title}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">
                    {listing.quantity ?? listing.amount}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{listing.unit}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2">
                    <span
                      className={cn(
                        'inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium',
                        listing.status === 'published' || listing.status === 'active'
                          ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
                          : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800'
                      )}
                    >
                      {listing.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-2">
                    {listing.status === 'draft' && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={busyId === listing.id || !canPublish}
                        title={
                          !canPublish
                            ? t('tables.companyMustBeVerified')
                            : t('tables.publishToMarketplace')
                        }
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          handlePublish(listing.id, listing.companyId);
                        }}
                        className="rounded bg-blue-600 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        {busyId === listing.id ? '...' : t('tables.publish')}
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })
          )}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/features/listings/components/marketplace-table.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useMarketplace } from '../hooks/use-marketplace';
import { cn } from '../../../lib/utils/cn';

export const MarketplaceTable = () => {
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useMarketplace();
  const router = useRouter();

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-4 text-center">Načítavam trhovisko...</div>;
  if (error) return <div className="p-4 text-red-500 text-center">Chyba pri načítaní trhoviska.</div>;

  const listings = (data as any)?.items ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
      <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-200 bg-white text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-gray-50">
          <tr>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Názov</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Množstvo</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Jednotka</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Lokalita</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Status</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
          {listings.length === 0 ? (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-gray-500">
                Žiadne cudzie publikované ponuky.
              </td>
            </tr>
          ) : (
            listings.map((listing: any) => (
              <tr
                key={listing.id}
                className="hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer"
                onClick={() => router.push(`/dashboard/listings/${listing.id}`)}
              >
                <td className="px-4 py-2 font-medium text-gray-900">{listing.title}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{listing.quantity ?? listing.amount}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{listing.unit}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{listing.location || '—'}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-2">
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      'inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium',
                      'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
                    )}
                  >
                    {listing.status}
                  </span>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))
          )}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useMatches } from '../hooks/use-matches';
import { Input } from '../../../components/ui/input';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/button';

export const MatchesPanel = () => {
  const [material, setMaterial] = useState('kov');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('kov');
  const { data, isLoading, error, refetch } = useMatches(query);
  const router = useRouter();
  const items = (data as any)?.items ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-end">
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-[160px]">
          <label className="mb-1 block text-xs text-gray-500">Typ materiálu</label>
          <Input
            value={material}
            onChange={(e) => setMaterial(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="napr. kov, železo"
          />
        </div>
        <Button
          type="button"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => {
            setQuery(material.trim());
            setTimeout(() => refetch(), 0);
          }}
        >
          Hľadať zhody
        </Button>
      </div>

      {isLoading && <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">Hľadám zhody...</div>}
      {error && <div className="text-sm text-red-600">Zhody sa nepodarilo načítať.</div>}

      {!isLoading && !error && (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
          <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-200 bg-white text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-50">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Názov</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Materiál</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Lokalita</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2 text-left font-medium text-gray-900">Množstvo</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
              {items.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={4} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-gray-500">
                    Žiadne zhody pre „{query}“.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                items.map((listing: any) => (
                  <tr
                    key={listing.id}
                    className="cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50"
                    onClick={() => router.push(`/dashboard/listings/${listing.id}`)}
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 font-medium text-gray-900">{listing.title}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{listing.materialType}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">{listing.location}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700">
                      {listing.quantity} {listing.unit}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { listingsApi } from '../api';
import { ListingInput } from '../schemas';

export const useCreateListing = () => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: ListingInput) => listingsApi.createListing(data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['listings'] });
    },
  });
};

import { z } from 'zod';

export const listingSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(3, 'Názov musí mať aspoň 3 znaky'),
  materialType: z.string().min(2, 'Typ materiálu je povinný'),
  quantity: z.number().min(0.01, 'Množstvo musí byť kladné'),
  unit: z.string().min(1, 'Jednotka je povinná'),
  price: z.number().optional(),
  currency: z.string().default('EUR'),
  condition: z.string().min(1, 'Stav je povinný'),
  location: z.string().min(2, 'Lokalita je povinná'),
  companyId: z.string().uuid('Vyber firmu'),
  description: z.string().optional(),
  imageUrl: z.string().url().optional().or(z.literal('')),
});

export type ListingInput = z.infer<typeof listingSchema>;

FILE: apps/web/src/features/recommendations/api.ts
================================================================================
import { config } from '../../lib/config';

export type RecommendationCandidate = {
  companyId: string;
  legalName: string;
  city: string;
  countryCode: string;
  buyerCapability: boolean;
  logisticsCapability: boolean;
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
};

export type RecommendationsResponse = {
  listingId: string;
  materialType: string;
  location: string;
  items: RecommendationCandidate[];
};

function errorDetail(body: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') return fallback;
  const detail = (body as { detail?: unknown }).detail;
  if (typeof detail === 'string') return detail;
  if (Array.isArray(detail) && detail[0] && typeof detail[0] === 'object') {
    const first = detail[0] as { msg?: string };
    return first.msg || fallback;
  }
  return fallback;
}

export async function getListingRecommendations(listingId: string): Promise<RecommendationsResponse> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/recommendations/listings/' + listingId, {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
  });
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new Error(errorDetail(body, 'HTTP ' + res.status + ' Recommendations request failed'));
  }
  return body as RecommendationsResponse;
}

export async function postListingViewed(listingId: string): Promise<void> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/events', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ eventType: 'listing_viewed', listingId }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) return;
}

'use client';

import { Suspense } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

function ChatDraftBannerInner() {
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const draft = params.get('draft') || '';
  if (!draft) return null;
  return (
    <div className="mb-4 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 bg-black p-4 text-[#f3e6c4]">
      <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Draft z odporucani</p>
      <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">{draft}</p>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="mt-3 rounded-full border border-[#d4af37]/50 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-[#d4af37]"
        onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(draft)}
      >
        Kopirovat do spravy
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

export function ChatDraftBanner() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <ChatDraftBannerInner />

FILE: apps/web/src/features/recommendations/components/freight-panel.tsx

'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { config } from '../../../lib/config';

type Carrier = {
  name: string;
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  source: string;
};

async function fetchCarriers(near: string): Promise<Carrier[]> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const res = await fetch(
    config.apiBaseUrl + '/recommendations/carriers?near=' + encodeURIComponent(near),
    {
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
      },
    },
  );
  const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new Error(typeof body.detail === 'string' ? body.detail : 'HTTP ' + res.status);
  }
  return (body.items || []) as Carrier[];
}

export function FreightPanel({ near }: { near?: string }) {
  const enabled = !!near;
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['company-carriers', near],
    queryFn: () => fetchCarriers(near as string),
    enabled,
  });

  return (
    <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 bg-black p-6 text-[#f3e6c4]">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Spedicia</p>
      <h2 className="mt-1 font-serif text-2xl">Dopravcovia v okoli</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">
        Firmy s logistikou pri {near || 'destinacii'}. Nie objednavka.
      </p>
      {!near && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Caka na mesto odberatela.</p>}
      {isLoading && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Hladam dopravcov…</p>}
      {error && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-red-300">{(error as Error).message}</p>}
      {data && data.length === 0 && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">V databaze nie je firma s logistikou pre toto miesto.</p>}
      <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
        {(data || []).map((row) => (
          <li key={row.name + row.lat} className="rounded-xl border border-[#d4af37]/25 p-3">
            <p className="font-semibold">{row.name}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#9a8b63]">{row.lat.toFixed(4)}, {row.lon.toFixed(4)} · {row.source}</p>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/features/recommendations/components/recommendations-panel.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRecommendations } from '../hooks/use-recommendations';

const REASON_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  verified_buyer: 'overeny odberatel',
  city_in_listing_location: 'mesto je v lokalite ponuky',
  logistics_capability: 'ma logistiku',
  material_accepted: 'prijima material',
  distance_le_50km: 'do 50 km',
  distance_le_150km: 'do 150 km',
};

type Props = {
  listingId: string;
  listingTitle?: string;
  listingLocation?: string;
  materialType?: string;
  onPickDestination?: (city: string) => void;
};

export function RecommendationsPanel({
  listingId,
  listingTitle,
  listingLocation,
  materialType,
  onPickDestination,
}: Props) {
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useRecommendations(listingId);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const first = data?.items?.[0];
    if (first && onPickDestination && first.city) onPickDestination(first.city);
  }, [data, onPickDestination]);

  function makeDraft(name: string, city: string) {
    setDraft(
      'Dobry den, ' +
        name +
        '. Mam zaujem overit odber ponuky "' +
        (listingTitle || listingId) +
        '" (' +
        (materialType || 'material') +
        ', ' +
        (listingLocation || 'lokalita neuvedena') +
        '). Ide o dopyt, nie o uzavrety obchod. Prosim o kratke potvrdenie zaujmu.',
    );
    if (onPickDestination && city) onPickDestination(city);
  }

  async function copyDraft() {
    if (!draft) return;
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(draft);
  }

  return (
    <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 bg-black p-6">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Odporucania</p>
      <h2 className="mt-1 font-serif text-2xl text-[#f3e6c4]">Mozni odberatelia</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Overeni odberatelia z existujucich dat. Obchod sa neuzatvara.</p>
      {isLoading && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Nacitavam…</p>}
      {error && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-red-300">{(error as Error).message}</p>}
      {data && data.items.length === 0 && (
        <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Ziadni overeni odberatelia.</p>
      )}
      {data && data.items.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="mt-4 space-y-3">
          {data.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.companyId} className="rounded-xl border border-[#d4af37]/25 bg-[#0b0b0b] p-4">
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
                <p className="font-semibold text-[#f3e6c4]">{item.legalName}</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-[#d4af37]">skore {item.score}</p>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#9a8b63]">{item.city}, {item.countryCode}</p>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[#9a8b63]">
                {item.reasons.map((reason) => REASON_LABEL[reason] || reason).join(' · ')}
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => makeDraft(item.legalName, item.city)}
                className="mt-3 rounded-full border border-[#d4af37]/50 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-[#d4af37]"
              >
                Navrhnut spravu a prepravu
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {draft && (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-[#d4af37]/25 p-4">
          <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Navrh spravy do chatu</p>
          <textarea
            value={draft}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-2 h-28 w-full rounded-lg border border-[#d4af37]/20 bg-black p-3 text-sm text-[#f3e6c4]"
          />
          <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
            <button type="button" onClick={copyDraft} className="rounded-full border border-[#d4af37]/50 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-[#d4af37]">
              Kopirovat
            </button>
            <a
              href={'/dashboard/chat?draft=' + encodeURIComponent(draft)}
              className="rounded-full border border-[#d4af37]/50 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-[#d4af37]"
            >
              Otvorit chat s draftom
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: apps/web/src/features/recommendations/components/report-button.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { config } from '@/lib/config';
import { tokenStore } from '@/lib/auth/token-store';

const REASONS = [
  { id: 'harassment', sk: 'Obtazovanie' },
  { id: 'fake', sk: 'Fejk ucet' },
  { id: 'spam', sk: 'Spam' },
] as const;

export function ReportButton({
  listingId,
  companyId,
}: {
  listingId?: string;
  companyId?: string;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState('');

  async function send(reason: string) {
    setErr('');
    const token = tokenStore.getAccessToken?.() || tokenStore.getToken?.();
    const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}/events`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        eventType: 'user_reported',
        listingId: listingId || null,
        companyId: companyId || null,
        payload: { reason },
      }),
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      setErr(String(body.detail || body.message || 'Nahradenie zlyhalo'));
      return;
    }
    setDone(true);
    setOpen(false);
  }

  if (done) {
    return <p className="text-xs text-[#cbb98a]">Nahlásené. Pozrie to admin.</p>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-3">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-[#c45c4a]"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
      >
        Nahlásiť
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {REASONS.map((r) => (
            <button
              key={r.id}
              type="button"
              className="rounded-full border border-[#c45c4a]/50 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-[#f3ead8]"
              onClick={() => send(r.id)}
            >
              {r.sk}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {err ? <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-red-300">{err}</p> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
