FILE: apps/web/src/features/recommendations/components/transport-panel.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { config } from '../../../lib/config';

type Props = { origin?: string; destination?: string };
type Option = {
  kind: string;
  label: string;
  distanceKm: number;
  durationMin: number;
  extraEur: number;
  totalEur: number;
  fuel: { fuelLiters: number; fuelEur: number; chargeableKm: number };
};
type RouteResult = {
  bestKind: string;
  mapUrl: string;
  origin: { label: string };
  destination: { label: string };
  options: Option[];
};

export function TransportPanel({ origin, destination }: Props) {
  const [route, setRoute] = useState<RouteResult | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!origin || !destination) return;
    const from = origin;
    const to = destination;
    let cancelled = false;
    async function run() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');
      try {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        const q =
          'origin=' + encodeURIComponent(from) +
          '&destination=' + encodeURIComponent(to) +
          '&consumption=28&fuel_price=1.49&roundtrip=true';
        const res = await fetch(config.apiBaseUrl + '/recommendations/route?' + q, {
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            ...(token ? { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}),
          },
        });
        const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (!res.ok) {
          throw new Error(typeof body.detail === 'string' ? body.detail : 'HTTP ' + res.status);
        }
        if (!cancelled) setRoute(body as RouteResult);
      } catch (err) {
        if (!cancelled) setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Vypocet zlyhal');
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }
    void run();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [origin, destination]);

  return (
    <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#d4af37]/35 bg-black p-6 text-[#f3e6c4]">
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#d4af37]">Preprava</p>
      <h2 className="mt-1 font-serif text-2xl">Auto trasa</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">
        {origin || '?'} → {destination || 'caka na odberatela'}
      </p>
      {loading && <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-[#cbb98a]">Pocitam OSRM…</p>}
      {error && <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</p>}
      {route && (
        <div className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          {route.options.map((opt) => (
            <div key={opt.kind} className="rounded-xl border border-[#d4af37]/25 p-3">
              <p className="text-[#d4af37]">{opt.label}{opt.kind === route.bestKind ? ' · odporucane' : ''}</p>
              <p>{opt.distanceKm} km · {opt.durationMin} min</p>
              <p>Nafta {opt.fuel.fuelLiters} l / {opt.fuel.fuelEur} EUR</p>
              {opt.extraEur ? <p>Znamka {opt.extraEur} EUR</p> : null}
              <p>Spolu {opt.totalEur} EUR</p>
            </div>
          ))}
          <a href={route.mapUrl} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="inline-block rounded-full border border-[#d4af37]/50 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-[#d4af37]">
            Navigacia
          </a>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/features/recommendations/hooks/use-recommendations.ts
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { getListingRecommendations } from '../api';

export function useRecommendations(listingId: string | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['recommendations', listingId],
    queryFn: () => getListingRecommendations(listingId as string),
    enabled: !!listingId,
    retry: 1,
  });
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/features/chat/hooks/use-send-message.ts
================================================================================
'use client';

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { http } from '@/lib/api/http';

export function useSendMessage(threadId: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: string) =>
      http.post(`/chat/threads/${threadId}/messages`, { body }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['chat-messages', threadId] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['chat-threads'] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['chat-unread'] });
    },
  });
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps/web/src/lib/i18n/context.tsx
================================================================================
'use client';

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, ReactNode } from 'react';
import sk from './messages/sk.json';
import en from './messages/en.json';
import ru from './messages/ru.json';
import fr from './messages/fr.json';
import de from './messages/de.json';

export type Locale = 'sk' | 'en' | 'ru' | 'fr' | 'de';

export const LOCALES: Locale[] = ['sk', 'en', 'ru', 'fr', 'de'];
export const LOCALE_LABELS: Record<Locale, string> = {
  sk: 'SK',
  en: 'EN',
  ru: 'RU',
  fr: 'FR',
  de: 'DE',
};
export const LOCALE_FLAGS: Record<Locale, string> = {
  sk: '🇸🇰',
  en: '🇬🇧',
  ru: '🇷🇺',
  fr: '🇫🇷',
  de: '🇩🇪',
};

const messages: Record<Locale, any> = { sk, en, ru, fr, de };
const STORAGE_KEY = 'cmep.locale';

type Ctx = {
  locale: Locale;
  setLocale: (l: Locale) => void;
  t: (key: string, fallback?: string) => string;
};

const LanguageContext = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);

function detectLocale(): Locale {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'sk';
  const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) as Locale | null;
  if (stored && LOCALES.includes(stored)) return stored;
  const nav = window.navigator.language.slice(0, 2).toLowerCase();
  if (LOCALES.includes(nav as Locale)) return nav as Locale;
  return 'sk';
}

function get(obj: any, path: string): any {
  return path.split('.').reduce((acc, k) => (acc ? acc[k] : undefined), obj);
}

export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [locale, setLocaleState] = useState<Locale>('sk');

  useEffect(() => {
    setLocaleState(detectLocale());
  }, []);

  const setLocale = (l: Locale) => {
    setLocaleState(l);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, l);
  };

  const t = (key: string, fallback?: string) => {
    const v = get(messages[locale], key);
    if (typeof v === 'string') return v;
    const f = get(messages.sk, key);
    if (typeof f === 'string') return f;
    return fallback ?? key;
  };

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ locale, setLocale, t }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useT() {
  const ctx = useContext(LanguageContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useT must be used inside LanguageProvider');
  return ctx;
}

    trading_name: str | None
    registration_number: str
    tax_identifier: str | None
    vat_identifier: str | None
    company_type: str
    email: str | None
    phone: str | None
    website_url: str | None

    address_line_1: str
    address_line_2: str | None

    postal_code: str

    longitude: float | None

    buyer_capability: bool

def to_company_dto(company: Company) -> CompanyDTO:
    return CompanyDTO(
        id=company.id,
        status=company.status.value,
        legal_name=company.legal_name,
        trading_name=company.trading_name,
        registration_number=company.registration_number,
        tax_identifier=company.tax_identifier,
        vat_identifier=getattr(company, "vat_identifier", None),
        company_type=company.company_type.value,
        email=company.email,
        phone=company.phone,
        website_url=company.website_url,
        country_code=company.country_code,
        address_line_1=company.address_line_1,
        address_line_2=company.address_line_2,
        city=company.city,
        postal_code=company.postal_code,
        latitude=company.latitude,
        longitude=company.longitude,
        seller_capability=company.seller_capability,
        buyer_capability=company.buyer_capability,
        logistics_capability=company.logistics_capability,
        verification_submitted_at=company.verification_submitted_at,
        verification_reviewed_at=company.verification_reviewed_at,
        verification_reviewed_by_user_id=company.verification_reviewed_by_user_id,
        verified_at=company.verified_at,
        verified_by_user_id=company.verified_by_user_id,
        rejected_at=company.rejected_at,
        rejected_by_user_id=company.rejected_by_user_id,
        created_at=company.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=company.created_by_user_id,
        updated_at=company.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=company.updated_by_user_id,
    )

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/companies/application/rpo.py

"""Lookup ICO in official SUSR RPO. No API key. CC-BY 4.0 SUSR."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

RPO_SEARCH = "https://api.statistics.sk/rpo/v1/search"


def normalize_ico(raw: str | None) -> str:
    digits = "".join(ch for ch in (raw or "") if ch.isdigit())
    return digits.zfill(8) if digits else ""


def lookup_ico(raw: str | None) -> dict:
    ico = normalize_ico(raw)
    if len(ico) != 8:
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "exists": False,
            "ico": ico or None,
            "legalName": None,
            "city": None,
            "reason": "ico_invalid",
        }
    url = f"{RPO_SEARCH}?{urlencode({'identifier': ico})}"
    req = Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "CMEP-RPO/1.0", "Accept": "application/json"})
    try:
        with urlopen(req, timeout=12) as resp:
            payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except HTTPError as exc:
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "exists": False,
            "ico": ico,
            "legalName": None,
            "city": None,
            "reason": f"http_{exc.code}",
        }
    except (URLError, TimeoutError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "exists": False,
            "ico": ico,
            "legalName": None,
            "city": None,
            "reason": "rpo_unavailable",
        }

    rows = payload.get("results") or []
    if not rows:
        return {
            "ok": True,
            "exists": False,
            "ico": ico,
            "legalName": None,
            "city": None,
            "reason": "not_found",
        }

    row = rows[0]
    names = row.get("fullNames") or []
    current = next((n for n in names if not n.get("validTo")), names[-1] if names else {})
    addrs = row.get("addresses") or []
    addr = next((a for a in addrs if not a.get("validTo")), addrs[0] if addrs else {})
    city = (
        ((addr.get("municipality") or {}).get("value"))
        if isinstance(addr.get("municipality"), dict)
        else None
    )
    return {
        "ok": True,
        "exists": True,
        "ico": ico,
        "legalName": current.get("value"),
        "city": city,
        "reason": "found",
    }

from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.companies.application.dto import CompanyDTO
from app.modules.companies.application.mappers import to_company_dto
from app.modules.companies.application.rpo import lookup_ico
from app.modules.companies.domain.entities import Company
from app.modules.companies.domain.enums import CompanyStatus, CompanyType
from app.modules.companies.domain.repositories import CompanyRepository
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.enums import (
    CompanyMembershipRole,
    CompanyMembershipStatus,
)
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import NotFoundDomainError


class CreateCompanyService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        repo: CompanyRepository,
        membership_repo,
        uuid_generator: UuidGenerator,
        clock: Clock,
    ):
        self.repo = repo
        self.membership_repo = membership_repo
        self.uuid_generator = uuid_generator
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        legal_name: str,
        trading_name: str | None,
        registration_number: str,
        tax_identifier: str | None,
        vat_identifier: str | None = None,
        company_type: str,
        email: str | None,
        phone: str | None,
        website_url: str | None,
        country_code: str,
        address_line_1: str,
        address_line_2: str | None,
        city: str,
        postal_code: str,
        latitude: float | None,
        longitude: float | None,
        seller_capability: bool,
        buyer_capability: bool,
        logistics_capability: bool,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> CompanyDTO:
        now = self.clock.now()
        rpo = lookup_ico(tax_identifier)
        if not rpo.get("exists"):
            raise ValueError("ICO_NOT_FOUND")
        company = Company(
            id=self.uuid_generator.new(),
            status=CompanyStatus.DRAFT,
            legal_name=legal_name,
            trading_name=trading_name,
            registration_number=registration_number,
            tax_identifier=tax_identifier,
            vat_identifier=vat_identifier,
            company_type=CompanyType(company_type),
            email=email,
            phone=phone,
            website_url=website_url,
            country_code=country_code.upper(),
            address_line_1=address_line_1,
            address_line_2=address_line_2,
            city=city,
            postal_code=postal_code,
            latitude=latitude,
            longitude=longitude,
            seller_capability=seller_capability,
            buyer_capability=buyer_capability,
            logistics_capability=logistics_capability,
            verification_submitted_at=None,
            verification_reviewed_at=None,
            verification_reviewed_by_user_id=None,
            verified_at=None,
            verified_by_user_id=None,
            rejected_at=None,
            rejected_by_user_id=None,
            created_at=now,
            created_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
            updated_at=now,
            updated_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
        )
        company.validate()
        await self.repo.add(company)

        if actor_user_id is not None:
            membership = CompanyMembership(
                id=self.uuid_generator.new(),
                status=CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE,
                company_id=company.id,
                user_id=actor_user_id,
                membership_role=CompanyMembershipRole.OWNER,
                is_primary_contact=True,
                invited_at=now,
                joined_at=now,
                left_at=None,
                created_at=now,
                created_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
                updated_at=now,
                updated_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
            )
            membership.validate()
            await self.membership_repo.add(membership)

        return to_company_dto(company)


class GetCompanyService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyRepository):
        self.repo = repo

    async def execute(self, company_id: UUID) -> CompanyDTO:
        company = await self.repo.get_by_id(company_id)
        if not company:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("Company not found")
        return to_company_dto(company)


class ListCompaniesService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyRepository):
        self.repo = repo

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        status: str | None,
        company_type: str | None,
        seller_capability: bool | None,
        buyer_capability: bool | None,
        logistics_capability: bool | None,
        q: str | None,
        page: int,
        page_size: int,
        company_ids: list | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[list[CompanyDTO], int]:
        companies, total = await self.repo.list(
            status=status,
            company_type=company_type,
            seller_capability=seller_capability,
            buyer_capability=buyer_capability,
            logistics_capability=logistics_capability,
            q=q,
            page=page,
            page_size=page_size,
            company_ids=company_ids,
        )
        return [to_company_dto(company) for company in companies], total


class UpdateCompanyService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        company_id: UUID,
        legal_name: str | None,
        trading_name: str | None,
        tax_identifier: str | None,
        vat_identifier: str | None = None,
        email: str | None,
        phone: str | None,
        website_url: str | None,
        address_line_1: str | None,
        address_line_2: str | None,
        city: str | None,
        postal_code: str | None,
        latitude: float | None,
        longitude: float | None,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> CompanyDTO:
        company = await self.repo.get_by_id(company_id)
        if not company:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("Company not found")
        if legal_name is not None:
            company.legal_name = legal_name
        if trading_name is not None:
            company.trading_name = trading_name
        if tax_identifier is not None:
            company.tax_identifier = tax_identifier
        if vat_identifier is not None:
            company.vat_identifier = vat_identifier
        if email is not None:
            company.email = email
        if phone is not None:
            company.phone = phone
        if website_url is not None:
            company.website_url = website_url
        if address_line_1 is not None:
            company.address_line_1 = address_line_1
        if address_line_2 is not None:
            company.address_line_2 = address_line_2
        if city is not None:
            company.city = city
        if postal_code is not None:
            company.postal_code = postal_code
        if latitude is not None:
            company.latitude = latitude
        if longitude is not None:
            company.longitude = longitude
        company.updated_at = self.clock.now()
        company.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        company.validate()
        await self.repo.update(company)
        return to_company_dto(company)


class TransitionCompanyStatusService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self, *, company_id: UUID, to_status: str, actor_user_id: UUID | None
    ) -> CompanyDTO:
        company = await self.repo.get_by_id(company_id)
        if not company:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("Company not found")

        target_status = CompanyStatus(to_status)
        company.transition_status(target_status)

        now = self.clock.now()
        if target_status == CompanyStatus.VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED:
            company.verification_submitted_at = now
        elif target_status == CompanyStatus.VERIFIED:
            company.verification_reviewed_at = now
            company.verification_reviewed_by_user_id = actor_user_id
            company.verified_at = now
            company.verified_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        elif target_status == CompanyStatus.REJECTED:
            company.verification_reviewed_at = now
            company.verification_reviewed_by_user_id = actor_user_id
            company.rejected_at = now
            company.rejected_by_user_id = actor_user_id

        company.updated_at = now
        company.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        await self.repo.update(company)
        return to_company_dto(company)

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/companies/domain/entities.py

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.companies.domain.enums import CompanyStatus, CompanyType
from app.shared.domain.base import AuditFields
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError
from app.shared.domain.status_transition import ensure_transition


@dataclass(slots=True)
class Company(AuditFields):
    status: CompanyStatus
    legal_name: str
    trading_name: str | None
    registration_number: str
    tax_identifier: str | None
    vat_identifier: str | None
    company_type: CompanyType
    email: str | None
    phone: str | None
    website_url: str | None
    country_code: str
    address_line_1: str
    address_line_2: str | None
    city: str
    postal_code: str
    latitude: float | None
    longitude: float | None
    seller_capability: bool
    buyer_capability: bool
    logistics_capability: bool
    verification_submitted_at: datetime | None
    verification_reviewed_at: datetime | None
    verification_reviewed_by_user_id: UUID | None
    verified_at: datetime | None
    verified_by_user_id: UUID | None
    rejected_at: datetime | None
    rejected_by_user_id: UUID | None

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not self.legal_name:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Company legal_name is required")
        if not self.registration_number:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Company registration_number is required")
        if len(self.country_code) != 2:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Company country_code must have ISO length 2")
        if not any([self.seller_capability, self.buyer_capability, self.logistics_capability]):
            raise ValidationDomainError("At least one company capability must be enabled")

    def transition_status(self, to_status: CompanyStatus) -> None:
        allowed = {
            CompanyStatus.DRAFT: {
                CompanyStatus.VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED,
                CompanyStatus.INACTIVE,
            },
            CompanyStatus.VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED: {
                CompanyStatus.VERIFIED,
                CompanyStatus.REJECTED,
                CompanyStatus.DRAFT,
            },
            CompanyStatus.VERIFIED: {CompanyStatus.SUSPENDED, CompanyStatus.INACTIVE},
            CompanyStatus.REJECTED: {
                CompanyStatus.DRAFT,
                CompanyStatus.VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED,
                CompanyStatus.INACTIVE,
            },
            CompanyStatus.SUSPENDED: {CompanyStatus.VERIFIED, CompanyStatus.INACTIVE},
            CompanyStatus.INACTIVE: set(),
        }
        ensure_transition(
            self.status.value, to_status.value, {s.value for s in allowed[self.status]}
        )
        self.status = to_status

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/companies/domain/enums.py

from enum import StrEnum


class CompanyStatus(StrEnum):
    DRAFT = "draft"
    VERIFICATION_SUBMITTED = "verification_submitted"
    VERIFIED = "verified"
    REJECTED = "rejected"
    SUSPENDED = "suspended"
    INACTIVE = "inactive"


class CompanyType(StrEnum):
    SELLER = "seller"
    BUYER = "buyer"
    SELLER_BUYER = "seller_buyer"
    LOGISTICS_PARTNER = "logistics_partner"

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/companies/domain/repositories.py
================================================================================
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.companies.infrastructure.models import CompanyModel


class CompanyRepository(ABC):
    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session
        self.model = CompanyModel

    @abstractmethod
    async def add(self, company) -> None: ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_id(self, company_id): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def list(
        self,
        *,
        status: str | None = None,
        company_type: str | None = None,
        seller_capability: bool | None = None,
        buyer_capability: bool | None = None,
        logistics_capability: bool | None = None,
        q: str | None = None,
        page: int = 1,
        page_size: int = 20,
        company_ids: list[int] | None = None,
    ): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def update(self, company) -> None: ...

from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.companies.domain.entities import Company
from app.modules.companies.domain.enums import CompanyStatus, CompanyType
from app.modules.companies.infrastructure.models import CompanyModel


def to_domain(model: CompanyModel) -> Company:
    return Company(
        id=UUID(model.id),
        status=CompanyStatus(model.status),
        legal_name=model.legal_name,
        trading_name=model.trading_name,
        registration_number=model.registration_number,
        tax_identifier=model.tax_identifier,
        vat_identifier=getattr(model, "vat_identifier", None),
        company_type=CompanyType(model.company_type),
        email=model.email,
        phone=model.phone,
        website_url=model.website_url,
        country_code=model.country_code,
        address_line_1=model.address_line_1,
        address_line_2=model.address_line_2,
        city=model.city,
        postal_code=model.postal_code,
        latitude=model.latitude,
        longitude=model.longitude,
        seller_capability=model.seller_capability,
        buyer_capability=model.buyer_capability,
        logistics_capability=model.logistics_capability,
        verification_submitted_at=model.verification_submitted_at,
        verification_reviewed_at=model.verification_reviewed_at,
        verification_reviewed_by_user_id=UUID(model.verification_reviewed_by_user_id)
        if model.verification_reviewed_by_user_id
        else None,
        verified_at=model.verified_at,
        verified_by_user_id=UUID(model.verified_by_user_id) if model.verified_by_user_id else None,
        rejected_at=model.rejected_at,
        rejected_by_user_id=UUID(model.rejected_by_user_id) if model.rejected_by_user_id else None,
        created_at=model.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=UUID(model.created_by_user_id) if model.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=model.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=UUID(model.updated_by_user_id) if model.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )


def to_model(entity: Company) -> CompanyModel:
    return CompanyModel(
        id=str(entity.id),
        status=entity.status.value,
        legal_name=entity.legal_name,
        trading_name=entity.trading_name,
        registration_number=entity.registration_number,
        tax_identifier=entity.tax_identifier,
        company_type=entity.company_type.value,
        email=entity.email,
        phone=entity.phone,
        website_url=entity.website_url,
        country_code=entity.country_code,
        address_line_1=entity.address_line_1,
        address_line_2=entity.address_line_2,
        city=entity.city,
        postal_code=entity.postal_code,
        latitude=entity.latitude,
        longitude=entity.longitude,
        seller_capability=entity.seller_capability,
        buyer_capability=entity.buyer_capability,
        logistics_capability=entity.logistics_capability,
        verification_submitted_at=entity.verification_submitted_at,
        verification_reviewed_at=entity.verification_reviewed_at,
        verification_reviewed_by_user_id=str(entity.verification_reviewed_by_user_id)
        if entity.verification_reviewed_by_user_id
        else None,
        verified_at=entity.verified_at,
        verified_by_user_id=str(entity.verified_by_user_id) if entity.verified_by_user_id else None,
        rejected_at=entity.rejected_at,
        rejected_by_user_id=str(entity.rejected_by_user_id) if entity.rejected_by_user_id else None,
        created_at=entity.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=str(entity.created_by_user_id) if entity.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=entity.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=str(entity.updated_by_user_id) if entity.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, Float, String, Text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base


class CompanyModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "companies"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False, index=True)
    legal_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    trading_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)
    registration_number: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(128), nullable=False, unique=True, index=True
    )
    tax_identifier: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)
    vat_identifier: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    company_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    email: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)
    phone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    website_url: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    country_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(2), nullable=False)
    address_line_1: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    address_line_2: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)
    city: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    postal_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    latitude: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    longitude: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    seller_capability: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    buyer_capability: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    logistics_capability: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    accepted_material_types: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    verification_submitted_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    verification_reviewed_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    verification_reviewed_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    verified_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    verified_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    rejected_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    rejected_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    created_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)

FILE: app/modules/companies/infrastructure/repositories.py

from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.companies.domain.entities import Company
from app.modules.companies.domain.repositories import CompanyRepository
from app.modules.companies.infrastructure.mappers import to_domain, to_model
from app.modules.companies.infrastructure.models import CompanyModel


class SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository(CompanyRepository):
    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session

    async def add(self, company: Company) -> None:
        self.session.add(to_model(company))
        await self.session.commit()

    async def get_by_id(self, company_id):
        stmt = select(CompanyModel).where(CompanyModel.id == str(company_id))
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list(
        self,
        *,
        status: str | None = None,
        company_type: str | None = None,
        seller_capability: bool | None = None,
        buyer_capability: bool | None = None,
        logistics_capability: bool | None = None,
        q: str | None = None,
        page: int = 1,
        page_size: int = 20,
        company_ids: list[int] | None = None,
    ):
        stmt = select(CompanyModel)
        count_stmt = select(func.count()).select_from(CompanyModel)

        if company_ids is not None:
            ids = [str(x) for x in company_ids]
            if not ids:
                return [], 0
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyModel.id.in_(ids))
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(CompanyModel.id.in_(ids))

        if status:
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyModel.status == status)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(CompanyModel.status == status)
        if company_type:
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyModel.company_type == company_type)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(CompanyModel.company_type == company_type)
        if seller_capability is not None:
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyModel.seller_capability == seller_capability)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(CompanyModel.seller_capability == seller_capability)
        if buyer_capability is not None:
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyModel.buyer_capability == buyer_capability)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(CompanyModel.buyer_capability == buyer_capability)
        if logistics_capability is not None:
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyModel.logistics_capability == logistics_capability)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(CompanyModel.logistics_capability == logistics_capability)
        if q:
            pattern = f"%{q}%"
            expr = or_(
                CompanyModel.legal_name.ilike(pattern),
                CompanyModel.trading_name.ilike(pattern),
                CompanyModel.registration_number.ilike(pattern),
            )
            stmt = stmt.where(expr)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(expr)

        stmt = (
            stmt.order_by(CompanyModel.created_at.desc())
            .offset((page - 1) * page_size)
            .limit(page_size)
        )
        rows = (await self.session.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()
        total = (await self.session.execute(count_stmt)).scalar_one()
        return [to_domain(row) for row in rows], int(total)

    async def update(self, company: Company) -> None:
        model = await self.session.get(CompanyModel, str(company.id))
        if model is None:
            return
        model.status = company.status.value
        model.legal_name = company.legal_name
        model.trading_name = company.trading_name
        model.tax_identifier = company.tax_identifier
        model.email = company.email
        model.phone = company.phone
        model.website_url = company.website_url
        model.address_line_1 = company.address_line_1
        model.address_line_2 = company.address_line_2
        model.city = company.city
        model.postal_code = company.postal_code
        model.latitude = company.latitude
        model.longitude = company.longitude
        model.verification_submitted_at = company.verification_submitted_at
        model.verification_reviewed_at = company.verification_reviewed_at
        model.verification_reviewed_by_user_id = (
            str(company.verification_reviewed_by_user_id)
            if company.verification_reviewed_by_user_id
            else None
        )
        model.verified_at = company.verified_at
        model.verified_by_user_id = (
            str(company.verified_by_user_id) if company.verified_by_user_id else None
        )
        model.rejected_at = company.rejected_at
        model.rejected_by_user_id = (
            str(company.rejected_by_user_id) if company.rejected_by_user_id else None
        )
        model.updated_at = company.updated_at
        model.updated_by_user_id = (
            str(company.updated_by_user_id) if company.updated_by_user_id else None
        )
        await self.session.commit()

from fastapi import Depends
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.companies.application.services import (
    CreateCompanyService,
    GetCompanyService,
    ListCompaniesService,
    TransitionCompanyStatusService,
    UpdateCompanyService,
)
from app.modules.companies.infrastructure.repositories import (
    SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.repositories import (
    SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.dependencies import (
    get_membership_repo,
)
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import get_db_session


def get_company_repo(
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
) -> SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository:
    return SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository(session)


def get_create_company_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository = Depends(get_company_repo),
    membership_repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo),
) -> CreateCompanyService:
    return CreateCompanyService(
        repo=repo,
        membership_repo=membership_repo,
        uuid_generator=UuidGenerator(),
        clock=Clock(),
    )


def get_get_company_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository = Depends(get_company_repo),
) -> GetCompanyService:
    return GetCompanyService(repo=repo)


def get_list_companies_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository = Depends(get_company_repo),
) -> ListCompaniesService:
    return ListCompaniesService(repo=repo)


def get_update_company_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository = Depends(get_company_repo),
) -> UpdateCompanyService:
    return UpdateCompanyService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())


def get_transition_company_status_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyRepository = Depends(get_company_repo),
) -> TransitionCompanyStatusService:
    return TransitionCompanyStatusService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())

from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query

from app.modules.companies.application.rpo import lookup_ico
from app.modules.companies.presentation.dependencies import (
    get_create_company_service,
    get_get_company_service,
    get_list_companies_service,
    get_transition_company_status_service,
    get_update_company_service,
)
from app.modules.companies.presentation.mappers import to_company_data
from app.modules.companies.presentation.schemas import (
    CompanyCreateRequest,
    CompanyResponse,
    CompanyStatusTransitionRequest,
    CompanyUpdateRequest,
    PaginatedCompaniesResponse,
    PaginationMeta,
)
from app.shared.application.side_effects import write_audit
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import get_db_session
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import (
    require_current_user,
)
from app.shared.presentation.authorization import (
    is_platform_admin,
    require_company_admin,
    require_company_member,
)
from app.shared.presentation.error_schemas import error_responses


def _is_admin(current_user) -> bool:
    """Platform admin. Deleguje na spolocnu definiciu opravnenia.

    Pozn.: tenant rola `admin` (firemne clenstvo) NIE je platform admin.
    """
    return is_platform_admin(current_user)


router = APIRouter(prefix="/companies", tags=["companies"])


@router.get("/rpo-check")
async def rpo_check(ico: str, current_user=Depends(require_current_user)):
    return lookup_ico(ico)


@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=PaginatedCompaniesResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def list_companies(
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    pageSize: int = Query(default=25, ge=1, le=100),
    status: str | None = None,
    companyType: str | None = None,
    sellerCapability: bool | None = None,
    buyerCapability: bool | None = None,
    logisticsCapability: bool | None = None,
    q: str | None = None,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_list_companies_service),
):
    if _is_admin(current_user):
        company_ids = None
    else:
        company_ids = [
            (m.get("companyId") if isinstance(m, dict) else getattr(m, "companyId", None))
            for m in (current_user.memberships or [])
        ]
        company_ids = [cid for cid in company_ids if cid is not None]
    items, total = await service.execute(
        status=status,
        company_type=companyType,
        seller_capability=sellerCapability,
        buyer_capability=buyerCapability,
        logistics_capability=logisticsCapability,
        q=q,
        page=page,
        page_size=pageSize,
        company_ids=company_ids,
    )
    return PaginatedCompaniesResponse(
        items=[to_company_data(item) for item in items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=pageSize, total=total),
    )


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=CompanyResponse,
    status_code=201,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def create_company(
    payload: CompanyCreateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_create_company_service),
):
    admin_only = {"verified", "rejected", "suspended", "inactive"}
    target = str(getattr(payload, "toStatus", "") or "").lower()
    if target in admin_only and not _is_admin(current_user):
        from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ForbiddenDomainError

        raise ForbiddenDomainError("Overenie firmy môže vykonať len administrátor.")

    try:
        dto = await service.execute(
            legal_name=payload.legalName,
            trading_name=payload.tradingName,
            registration_number=payload.registrationNumber,
            tax_identifier=payload.taxIdentifier,
            vat_identifier=getattr(payload, "vatIdentifier", None),
            company_type=payload.companyType,
            email=payload.email,
            phone=payload.phone,
            website_url=str(payload.websiteUrl) if payload.websiteUrl else None,
            country_code=payload.countryCode,
            address_line_1=payload.addressLine1,
            address_line_2=payload.addressLine2,
            city=payload.city,
            postal_code=payload.postalCode,
            latitude=payload.latitude,
            longitude=payload.longitude,
            seller_capability=payload.sellerCapability,
            buyer_capability=payload.buyerCapability,
            logistics_capability=payload.logisticsCapability,
            actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        if str(exc) == "ICO_NOT_FOUND":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="IČO v RPO neexistuje") from exc
        raise
    return CompanyResponse(data=to_company_data(dto))


@router.get(
    "/{company_id}",
    response_model=CompanyResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 500),
)
async def get_company(
    company_id: UUID,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_get_company_service),
):
    require_company_member(current_user, company_id, action="access")
    dto = await service.execute(company_id)
    return CompanyResponse(data=to_company_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/{company_id}",
    response_model=CompanyResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def update_company(
    company_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyUpdateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_update_company_service),
):
    require_company_admin(current_user, company_id)
    dto = await service.execute(
        company_id=company_id,
        legal_name=payload.legalName,
        trading_name=payload.tradingName,
        tax_identifier=payload.taxIdentifier,
        vat_identifier=getattr(payload, "vatIdentifier", None),
        email=payload.email,
        phone=payload.phone,
        website_url=str(payload.websiteUrl) if payload.websiteUrl else None,
        address_line_1=payload.addressLine1,
        address_line_2=payload.addressLine2,
        city=payload.city,
        postal_code=payload.postalCode,
        latitude=payload.latitude,
        longitude=payload.longitude,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return CompanyResponse(data=to_company_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/{company_id}/status",
    response_model=CompanyResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def transition_company_status(
    company_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyStatusTransitionRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_transition_company_status_service),
    session=Depends(get_db_session),
):
    from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ForbiddenDomainError

    target = str(getattr(payload, "toStatus", None) or getattr(payload, "to_status", "")).lower()
    admin_only = {"verified", "rejected", "suspended", "inactive"}

    if target in admin_only:
        if not _is_admin(current_user):
            raise ForbiddenDomainError("Overenie firmy moze vykonat len administrator platformy.")
    else:
        # Neadministrativny prechod (napr. verification_submitted) vyzaduje
        # tenant opravnenie v ramci danej firmy.
        require_company_admin(current_user, company_id)

    dto = await service.execute(
        company_id=company_id,
        to_status=payload.toStatus,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    actor = getattr(getattr(current_user, "user", None), "id", None)
    import contextlib

    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
        await write_audit(
            session=session,
            actor_user_id=actor,
            action="company.status_changed",
            target_type="company",
            target_id=company_id,
            details=f"toStatus={payload.toStatus}",
        )
    try:
        from app.shared.application.side_effects import notify_company_members

        tl = str(payload.toStatus).lower()
        if tl == "verified":
            await notify_company_members(
                session=session,
                company_id=company_id,
                title="Firma overena",
                message="Vasa firma bola overena administratorom.",
                notif_type="company_verified",
                related_entity_id=company_id,
                exclude_user_id=actor,
            )
        elif tl == "rejected":
            await notify_company_members(
                session=session,
                company_id=company_id,
                title="Firma zamietnuta",
                message="Overenie vasej firmy bolo zamietnute.",
                notif_type="company_rejected",
                related_entity_id=company_id,
                exclude_user_id=actor,
            )
    except Exception:
        pass
    return CompanyResponse(data=to_company_data(dto))

from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from pydantic import BaseModel, HttpUrl

from app.modules.companies.domain.enums import CompanyStatus, CompanyType


class CompanyCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    legalName: str
    registrationNumber: str
    companyType: CompanyType
    countryCode: str
    addressLine1: str
    city: str
    postalCode: str
    sellerCapability: bool
    buyerCapability: bool
    logisticsCapability: bool
    acceptedMaterialTypes: str | None = None
    tradingName: str | None = None
    taxIdentifier: str | None = None
    vatIdentifier: str | None = None
    email: str | None = None
    phone: str | None = None
    websiteUrl: HttpUrl | None = None
    addressLine2: str | None = None
    latitude: float | None = None
    longitude: float | None = None
    acceptedMaterialTypes: str | None = None


class CompanyUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    legalName: str | None = None
    tradingName: str | None = None
    taxIdentifier: str | None = None
    vatIdentifier: str | None = None
    email: str | None = None
    phone: str | None = None
    websiteUrl: HttpUrl | None = None
    addressLine1: str | None = None
    addressLine2: str | None = None
    city: str | None = None
    postalCode: str | None = None
    latitude: float | None = None
    longitude: float | None = None


class CompanyData(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    status: CompanyStatus
    legalName: str
    tradingName: str | None
    registrationNumber: str
    taxIdentifier: str | None
    vatIdentifier: str | None = None
    companyType: CompanyType
    email: str | None
    phone: str | None
    websiteUrl: str | None
    countryCode: str
    addressLine1: str
    addressLine2: str | None
    city: str
    postalCode: str
    latitude: float | None
    longitude: float | None
    sellerCapability: bool
    buyerCapability: bool
    logisticsCapability: bool
    acceptedMaterialTypes: str | None = None
    verificationSubmittedAt: datetime | None
    verificationReviewedAt: datetime | None
    verificationReviewedByUserId: UUID | None
    verifiedAt: datetime | None
    verifiedByUserId: UUID | None
    rejectedAt: datetime | None
    rejectedByUserId: UUID | None
    createdAt: datetime
    createdByUserId: UUID | None
    updatedAt: datetime
    updatedByUserId: UUID | None


class CompanyResponse(BaseModel):
    data: CompanyData


class PaginationMeta(BaseModel):
    page: int
    pageSize: int
    total: int


class PaginatedCompaniesResponse(BaseModel):
    items: list[CompanyData]
    meta: PaginationMeta


class CompanyStatusTransitionRequest(BaseModel):
    toStatus: CompanyStatus

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/company_memberships/application/services.py
================================================================================
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.company_memberships.application.dto import CompanyMembershipDTO
from app.modules.company_memberships.application.mappers import to_membership_dto
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.enums import (
    CompanyMembershipRole,
    CompanyMembershipStatus,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.repositories import (
    CompanyMembershipRepository,
)
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import NotFoundDomainError


class CreateCompanyMembershipService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        repo: CompanyMembershipRepository,
        uuid_generator: UuidGenerator,
        clock: Clock,
    ):
        self.repo = repo
        self.uuid_generator = uuid_generator
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        company_id: UUID,
        user_id: UUID,
        membership_role: str,
        is_primary_contact: bool,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> CompanyMembershipDTO:
        now = self.clock.now()
        membership = CompanyMembership(
            id=self.uuid_generator.new(),
            status=CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE,
            company_id=company_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            membership_role=CompanyMembershipRole(membership_role),
            is_primary_contact=is_primary_contact,
            invited_at=now,
            joined_at=now,
            left_at=None,
            created_at=now,
            created_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
            updated_at=now,
            updated_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
        )
        membership.validate()
        await self.repo.add(membership)
        return to_membership_dto(membership)


class ListCompanyMembershipsService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyMembershipRepository):
        self.repo = repo

    async def execute(self, *, company_id: UUID, status: str | None) -> list[CompanyMembershipDTO]:
        items = await self.repo.list_by_company_id(company_id, status)
        return [to_membership_dto(item) for item in items]


class UpdateCompanyMembershipService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyMembershipRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        membership_id: UUID,
        membership_role: str | None,
        is_primary_contact: bool | None,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> CompanyMembershipDTO:
        membership = await self.repo.get_by_id(membership_id)
        if not membership:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("Company membership not found")
        if membership_role is not None:
            membership.membership_role = CompanyMembershipRole(membership_role)
        if is_primary_contact is not None:
            membership.is_primary_contact = is_primary_contact
        membership.updated_at = self.clock.now()
        membership.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        await self.repo.update(membership)
        return to_membership_dto(membership)


class TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyMembershipRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self, *, membership_id: UUID, to_status: str, actor_user_id: UUID | None
    ) -> CompanyMembershipDTO:
        membership = await self.repo.get_by_id(membership_id)
        if not membership:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("Company membership not found")
        membership.transition_status(CompanyMembershipStatus(to_status))
        membership.updated_at = self.clock.now()
        membership.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        await self.repo.update(membership)
        return to_membership_dto(membership)

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/company_memberships/domain/entities.py
================================================================================
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.enums import (
    CompanyMembershipRole,
    CompanyMembershipStatus,
)
from app.shared.domain.base import AuditFields
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError
from app.shared.domain.status_transition import ensure_transition


@dataclass(slots=True, kw_only=True)
class CompanyMembership(AuditFields):
    status: CompanyMembershipStatus
    company_id: UUID
    user_id: UUID
    membership_role: CompanyMembershipRole
    is_primary_contact: bool
    invited_at: datetime | None = None
    joined_at: datetime | None = None
    left_at: datetime | None = None

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not self.company_id:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Membership company_id is required")
        if not self.user_id:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Membership user_id is required")

    def transition_status(self, to_status: CompanyMembershipStatus) -> None:
        allowed = {
            CompanyMembershipStatus.INVITED: {
                CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE,
                CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED,
            },
            CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE: {
                CompanyMembershipStatus.INACTIVE,
                CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED,
            },
            CompanyMembershipStatus.INACTIVE: {
                CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE,
                CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED,
            },
            CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED: set(),
        }
        ensure_transition(
            self.status.value, to_status.value, {s.value for s in allowed[self.status]}
        )
        self.status = to_status

class CompanyMembershipStatus(StrEnum):
    INVITED = "invited"
    ACTIVE = "active"
    INACTIVE = "inactive"
    REMOVED = "removed"


class CompanyMembershipRole(StrEnum):
    OWNER = "owner"
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MEMBER = "member"
    COMPLIANCE_CONTACT = "compliance_contact"
    SALES_CONTACT = "sales_contact"
    LOGISTICS_CONTACT = "logistics_contact"
    VIEWER = "viewer"

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership


class CompanyMembershipRepository(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    async def add(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_id(self, membership_id: UUID) -> CompanyMembership | None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def list_by_company_id(
        self, company_id: UUID, status: str | None = None
    ) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def list_by_user_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def update(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.enums import (
    CompanyMembershipRole,
    CompanyMembershipStatus,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.models import CompanyMembershipModel


def to_domain(model: CompanyMembershipModel) -> CompanyMembership:
    return CompanyMembership(
        id=UUID(model.id),
        status=CompanyMembershipStatus(model.status),
        company_id=UUID(model.company_id),
        user_id=UUID(model.user_id),
        membership_role=CompanyMembershipRole(model.membership_role),
        is_primary_contact=model.is_primary_contact,
        invited_at=model.invited_at,
        joined_at=model.joined_at,
        left_at=model.left_at,
        created_at=model.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=UUID(model.created_by_user_id) if model.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=model.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=UUID(model.updated_by_user_id) if model.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )


def to_model(entity: CompanyMembership) -> CompanyMembershipModel:
    return CompanyMembershipModel(
        id=str(entity.id),
        status=entity.status.value,
        company_id=str(entity.company_id),
        user_id=str(entity.user_id),
        membership_role=entity.membership_role.value,
        is_primary_contact=entity.is_primary_contact,
        invited_at=entity.invited_at,
        joined_at=entity.joined_at,
        left_at=entity.left_at,
        created_at=entity.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=str(entity.created_by_user_id) if entity.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=entity.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=str(entity.updated_by_user_id) if entity.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base


class CompanyMembershipModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "company_memberships"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False, index=True)
    company_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False, index=True)
    user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False, index=True)
    membership_role: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    is_primary_contact: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    invited_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    joined_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    left_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    created_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)

FILE: app/modules/company_memberships/infrastructure/repositories.py

from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.repositories import (
    CompanyMembershipRepository,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.mappers import to_domain, to_model
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.models import CompanyMembershipModel


class SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository(CompanyMembershipRepository):
    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session

    async def add(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        self.session.add(to_model(membership))
        await self.session.commit()

    async def get_by_id(self, membership_id: UUID) -> CompanyMembership | None:
        stmt = select(CompanyMembershipModel).where(CompanyMembershipModel.id == str(membership_id))
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list_by_company_id(
        self, company_id: UUID, status: str | None = None
    ) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        stmt = select(CompanyMembershipModel).where(
            CompanyMembershipModel.company_id == str(company_id)
        )
        if status:
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyMembershipModel.status == status)
        stmt = stmt.order_by(CompanyMembershipModel.created_at.desc())
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        return [to_domain(item) for item in result.scalars().all()]

    async def list_by_user_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        stmt = select(CompanyMembershipModel).where(CompanyMembershipModel.user_id == str(user_id))
        stmt = stmt.order_by(CompanyMembershipModel.created_at.desc())
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        return [to_domain(item) for item in result.scalars().all()]

    async def update(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        model = await self.session.get(CompanyMembershipModel, str(membership.id))
        if model is None:
            return
        model.status = membership.status.value
        model.membership_role = membership.membership_role.value
        model.is_primary_contact = membership.is_primary_contact
        model.invited_at = membership.invited_at
        model.joined_at = membership.joined_at
        model.left_at = membership.left_at
        model.updated_at = membership.updated_at
        model.updated_by_user_id = (
            str(membership.updated_by_user_id) if membership.updated_by_user_id else None
        )
        await self.session.commit()

from fastapi import Depends
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.company_memberships.application.services import (
    CreateCompanyMembershipService,
    ListCompanyMembershipsService,
    TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService,
    UpdateCompanyMembershipService,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.repositories import (
    SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository,
)
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import get_db_session


def get_membership_repo(
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
) -> SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository:
    return SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository(session)


def get_create_membership_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo),
) -> CreateCompanyMembershipService:
    return CreateCompanyMembershipService(repo=repo, uuid_generator=UuidGenerator(), clock=Clock())


def get_list_memberships_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo),
) -> ListCompanyMembershipsService:
    return ListCompanyMembershipsService(repo=repo)


def get_update_membership_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo),
) -> UpdateCompanyMembershipService:
    return UpdateCompanyMembershipService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())


def get_transition_membership_status_service(
    repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo),
) -> TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService:
    return TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())

from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.dependencies import (
    get_create_membership_service,
    get_list_memberships_service,
    get_membership_repo,
    get_transition_membership_status_service,
    get_update_membership_service,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.mappers import to_membership_data
from app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.schemas import (
    CompanyMembershipCreateRequest,
    CompanyMembershipResponse,
    CompanyMembershipStatusTransitionRequest,
    CompanyMembershipUpdateRequest,
    PaginatedCompanyMembershipsResponse,
    PaginationMeta,
)
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import NotFoundDomainError
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_current_user
from app.shared.presentation.authorization import (
    require_company_admin,
    require_company_member,
)
from app.shared.presentation.error_schemas import error_responses

router = APIRouter(tags=["company-memberships"])


async def _membership_company_id(repo, membership_id: UUID) -> UUID:
    """Najdi company_id clenstva, aby sa dalo overit opravnenie voci firme."""
    membership = await repo.get_by_id(membership_id)
    if membership is None:
        raise NotFoundDomainError("Company membership not found")
    return membership.company_id


@router.get(
    "/companies/{company_id}/memberships",
    response_model=PaginatedCompanyMembershipsResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def list_company_memberships(
    company_id: UUID,
    status: str | None = None,
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    pageSize: int = Query(default=100, ge=1, le=100),
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_list_memberships_service),
):
    # Zoznam clenov firmy vidi len clen danej firmy (alebo platform admin).
    require_company_member(current_user, company_id, action="access")

    items = await service.execute(company_id=company_id, status=status)
    start = (page - 1) * pageSize
    slice_items = items[start : start + pageSize]
    return PaginatedCompanyMembershipsResponse(
        items=[to_membership_data(item) for item in slice_items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=pageSize, total=len(items)),
    )


@router.post(
    "/companies/{company_id}/memberships",
    response_model=CompanyMembershipResponse,
    status_code=201,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def create_company_membership(
    company_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyMembershipCreateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_create_membership_service),
):
    # Pridavat clenov smie len tenant admin danej firmy (alebo platform admin).
    require_company_admin(current_user, company_id, action="manage members of")

    dto = await service.execute(
        company_id=company_id,
        user_id=payload.userId,
        membership_role=payload.membershipRole,
        is_primary_contact=payload.isPrimaryContact,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return CompanyMembershipResponse(data=to_membership_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/company-memberships/{membership_id}",
    response_model=CompanyMembershipResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def update_company_membership(
    membership_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyMembershipUpdateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_update_membership_service),
    repo=Depends(get_membership_repo),
):
    company_id = await _membership_company_id(repo, membership_id)
    require_company_admin(current_user, company_id, action="manage members of")

    dto = await service.execute(
        membership_id=membership_id,
        membership_role=payload.membershipRole,
        is_primary_contact=payload.isPrimaryContact,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return CompanyMembershipResponse(data=to_membership_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/company-memberships/{membership_id}/status",
    response_model=CompanyMembershipResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def transition_company_membership_status(
    membership_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyMembershipStatusTransitionRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_transition_membership_status_service),
    repo=Depends(get_membership_repo),
):
    company_id = await _membership_company_id(repo, membership_id)
    require_company_admin(current_user, company_id, action="manage members of")

    dto = await service.execute(
        membership_id=membership_id,
        to_status=payload.toStatus,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return CompanyMembershipResponse(data=to_membership_data(dto))

"""listings application services."""

from app.shared.domain.exceptions import NotFoundDomainError

from ..domain.entities import ListingsEntity
from ..domain.repositories import ListingsRepository


class ListingsService:
    """Listings service."""

    def __init__(self, repository: ListingsRepository):
        self.repository = repository

    async def get_by_id(self, id: str) -> ListingsEntity | None:
        return await self.repository.get_by_id(id)

    async def list_all(
        self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20, company_ids=None
    ) -> list[ListingsEntity]:
        return await self.repository.list_all(page, limit, company_ids=company_ids)

    async def count_all(self, company_ids=None) -> int:
        return await self.repository.count_all(company_ids=company_ids)

    async def list_marketplace(self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20, exclude_company_ids=None):
        return await self.repository.list_marketplace(
            page, limit, exclude_company_ids=exclude_company_ids
        )

    async def count_marketplace(self, exclude_company_ids=None) -> int:
        return await self.repository.count_marketplace(exclude_company_ids=exclude_company_ids)

    async def create(self, entity: ListingsEntity) -> ListingsEntity:
        entity.validate()
        return await self.repository.create(entity)

    async def update(self, id: str, entity: ListingsEntity) -> ListingsEntity:
        existing = await self.repository.get_by_id(id)
        if existing is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {id} not found")
        entity.id = existing.id
        entity.created_at = existing.created_at
        entity.created_by_user_id = existing.created_by_user_id
        entity.validate()
        return await self.repository.update(entity)

    async def publish(self, id: str):
        """draft → published, len ak firma listing-u je verified."""
        from datetime import datetime

        from app.modules.listings.domain.enums import ListingsStatus
        from app.shared.domain.exceptions import (
            NotFoundDomainError,
            ValidationDomainError,
        )

        existing = await self.repository.get_by_id(id)
        if existing is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {id} not found")

        # overenie firmy cez raw SQL v repo
        status_val = await self.repository.get_company_status(existing.company_id)
        if status_val != "verified":
            raise ValidationDomainError(
                "Publikovať môže len overená firma (status verified). Najprv over firmu."
            )
        if not (getattr(existing, "location", None) or "").strip():
            raise ValidationDomainError("Publikovať môžeš len ponuku s lokalitou.")

        existing.status = ListingsStatus.PUBLISHED
        existing.updated_at = datetime.now()
        return await self.repository.update(existing)

    async def delete(self, id: str) -> None:
        existing = await self.repository.get_by_id(id)
        if existing is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {id} not found")
        await self.repository.delete(id)

    async def find_matches(self, *, material_type: str, exclude_company_ids, limit: int = 20):
        return await self.repository.find_matches(
            material_type=material_type,
            exclude_company_ids=exclude_company_ids or [],
            limit=limit,
        )

    async def record_view(self, listing_id, user_id) -> None:
        """Zapise jedno zobrazenie ponuky konkretnym userom."""
        existing = await self.repository.get_by_id(listing_id)
        if existing is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {listing_id} not found")
        await self.repository.record_view(listing_id, user_id)

    async def get_stats(self, listing_id: str, owner_company_ids=None) -> dict:
        """Vrati {views, unique_viewers}. Iba vlastnik (company v zozname) to smie vidiet."""
        existing = await self.repository.get_by_id(listing_id)
        if existing is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {listing_id} not found")
        owner_ids = {str(x) for x in (owner_company_ids or []) if x is not None}
        if owner_ids and str(existing.company_id) not in owner_ids:
            from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError

            raise ValidationDomainError("Statistiky moze vidiet len vlastnik ponuky.")
        stats = await self.repository.get_listing_stats(listing_id)
        return {"views": stats["views"], "uniqueViewers": stats["unique_viewers"]}

"""listings domain entities."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.modules.listings.domain.enums import ListingsStatus
from app.shared.domain.base import AuditFields
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ListingsEntity(AuditFields):
    """Listing entity - ponuka druhotneho materialu."""

    title: str
    material_type: str
    quantity: float
    unit: str
    condition: str
    location: str
    company_id: str
    status: ListingsStatus = ListingsStatus.DRAFT
    price: float | None = None
    currency: str = "EUR"
    description: str | None = None
    image_url: str | None = None

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not self.title:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Listing title is required")
        if not self.material_type:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Listing material_type is required")
        if self.quantity <= 0:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Listing quantity must be positive")
        if not self.company_id:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Listing company_id is required")

    PUBLISHED = "published"
    RESERVED = "reserved"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    ARCHIVED = "archived"

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    pass


class ListingsRepository(ABC):
    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session
        from app.modules.listings.infrastructure.models import ListingsModel as _LM

        self.model = _LM

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_id(self, id: str): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def list_all(self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20, company_ids=None): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def count_all(self, company_ids=None) -> int: ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def list_marketplace(self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20, exclude_company_ids=None): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def count_marketplace(self, exclude_company_ids=None) -> int: ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_company_status(self, company_id): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def create(self, entity): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def update(self, entity): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def delete(self, id: str) -> None: ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def find_matches(self, *, material_type: str, exclude_company_ids, limit: int = 20): ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def record_view(self, listing_id, user_id) -> None: ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_listing_stats(self, listing_id) -> dict: ...
