"""listings infrastructure mappers - domain entity <-> SQLAlchemy model."""

from app.modules.listings.domain.entities import ListingsEntity
from app.modules.listings.domain.enums import ListingsStatus
from app.modules.listings.infrastructure.models import ListingsModel


def to_domain(model: ListingsModel) -> ListingsEntity:
    return ListingsEntity(
        id=model.id,
        title=model.title,
        material_type=model.material_type,
        quantity=model.quantity,
        unit=model.unit,
        price=model.price,
        currency=model.currency,
        condition=model.condition,
        location=model.location,
        status=ListingsStatus(model.status),
        company_id=str(model.company_id) if model.company_id else None,
        description=model.description,
        image_url=getattr(model, "image_url", None),
        created_at=model.created_at,
        updated_at=model.updated_at,
        created_by_user_id=model.created_by_user_id,
        updated_by_user_id=model.updated_by_user_id,
    )


def to_model(entity: ListingsEntity) -> ListingsModel:
    return ListingsModel(
        id=entity.id,
        title=entity.title,
        material_type=entity.material_type,
        quantity=entity.quantity,
        unit=entity.unit,
        price=entity.price,
        currency=entity.currency,
        condition=entity.condition,
        location=entity.location,
        status=entity.status.value,
        company_id=entity.company_id,
        description=entity.description,
        image_url=getattr(entity, "image_url", None),
        created_at=entity.created_at,
        updated_at=entity.updated_at,
        created_by_user_id=entity.created_by_user_id,
        updated_by_user_id=entity.updated_by_user_id,
    )

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/listings/infrastructure/models.py
================================================================================
"""listings database models."""

import uuid
from datetime import datetime

import sqlalchemy as sa
from sqlalchemy import Column, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Index, String, Text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base


class ListingsModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "listings"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    title = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    material_type = Column(String(120), nullable=False)
    quantity = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    unit = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    price = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    currency = Column(String(8), nullable=False, default="EUR")
    condition = Column(String(120), nullable=False)
    location = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    latitude = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    longitude = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    legal_regime = Column(String(32), nullable=True)
    status = Column(String(32), nullable=False, default="draft")
    company_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=False)
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    image_url = Column(String(512), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=lambda: datetime.now())
    updated_at = Column(
        DateTime,
        default=lambda: datetime.now(),
        onupdate=lambda: datetime.now(),
    )
    created_by_user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=True)
    updated_by_user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=True)


class ListingViewModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "listing_views"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, server_default=sa.text("gen_random_uuid()"))
    listing_id = Column(
        UUID(as_uuid=True),
        ForeignKey("listings.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        nullable=False,
        index=True,
    )
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=False)
    viewed_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("now()"))

    __table_args__ = (Index("ix_listing_views_listing_user", "listing_id", "user_id"),)

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/listings/infrastructure/repositories.py
================================================================================
"""listings repository implementations."""

from sqlalchemy import delete as sql_delete
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from ..domain.entities import ListingsEntity
from ..domain.repositories import ListingsRepository
from .mappers import to_domain, to_model
from .models import ListingsModel


class ListingsRepositoryImpl(ListingsRepository):
    """Listings repository implementation."""

    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session

    async def get_by_id(self, id: str) -> ListingsEntity | None:
        result = await self.session.execute(select(ListingsModel).where(ListingsModel.id == id))
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list_all(
        self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20, company_ids=None
    ) -> list[ListingsEntity]:
        offset = (page - 1) * limit
        stmt = select(ListingsModel)
        if company_ids is not None:
            ids = [str(x) for x in company_ids]
            if not ids:
                return []
            stmt = stmt.where(ListingsModel.company_id.in_(ids))
        stmt = stmt.order_by(ListingsModel.created_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(limit)
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        models = result.scalars().all()
        return [to_domain(m) for m in models]

    async def count_all(self, company_ids=None) -> int:
        stmt = select(func.count()).select_from(ListingsModel)
        if company_ids is not None:
            ids = [str(x) for x in company_ids]
            if not ids:
                return 0
            stmt = stmt.where(ListingsModel.company_id.in_(ids))
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        return int(result.scalar_one())

    async def list_marketplace(
        self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20, exclude_company_ids=None
    ) -> list[ListingsEntity]:
        offset = (page - 1) * limit
        stmt = select(ListingsModel).where(ListingsModel.status == "published")
        if exclude_company_ids:
            ids = [str(x) for x in exclude_company_ids if x is not None]
            if ids:
                stmt = stmt.where(ListingsModel.company_id.notin_(ids))
        stmt = stmt.order_by(ListingsModel.created_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(limit)
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        models = result.scalars().all()
        return [to_domain(m) for m in models]

    async def count_marketplace(self, exclude_company_ids=None) -> int:
        stmt = (
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(ListingsModel)
            .where(ListingsModel.status == "published")
        )
        if exclude_company_ids:
            ids = [str(x) for x in exclude_company_ids if x is not None]
            if ids:
                stmt = stmt.where(ListingsModel.company_id.notin_(ids))
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        return int(result.scalar_one())

    async def get_company_status(self, company_id) -> str | None:
        from sqlalchemy import text

        if company_id is None:
            return None
        result = await self.session.execute(
            text("SELECT status FROM companies WHERE id = :id"),
            {"id": str(company_id)},
        )
        row = result.first()
        return row[0] if row else None

    async def create(self, entity: ListingsEntity) -> ListingsEntity:
        model = to_model(entity)
        self.session.add(model)
        await self.session.commit()
        await self.session.refresh(model)
        return to_domain(model)

    async def update(self, entity: ListingsEntity) -> ListingsEntity:
        model = await self.session.get(ListingsModel, entity.id)
        if model is None:
            raise ValueError("Entity not found")
        model.title = entity.title
        model.material_type = entity.material_type
        model.quantity = entity.quantity
        model.unit = entity.unit
        model.price = entity.price
        model.currency = entity.currency
        model.condition = entity.condition
        model.location = entity.location
        model.status = entity.status.value
        model.description = entity.description
        model.updated_at = entity.updated_at
        model.updated_by_user_id = entity.updated_by_user_id
        await self.session.commit()
        await self.session.refresh(model)
        return to_domain(model)

    async def delete(self, id: str) -> None:
        await self.session.execute(sql_delete(ListingsModel).where(ListingsModel.id == id))
        await self.session.commit()

    async def find_matches(self, *, material_type: str, exclude_company_ids, limit: int = 20):
        """Published listings with same material_type, excluding given companies."""
        stmt = select(ListingsModel).where(ListingsModel.status == "published")
        if material_type:
            stmt = stmt.where(ListingsModel.material_type.ilike(f"%{material_type.strip()}%"))
        if exclude_company_ids:
            stmt = stmt.where(~ListingsModel.company_id.in_(list(exclude_company_ids)))
        stmt = stmt.order_by(ListingsModel.created_at.desc()).limit(limit)
        rows = (await self.session.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()
        return [to_domain(r) for r in rows]

    async def record_view(self, listing_id, user_id) -> None:
        import uuid as _uuid
        from datetime import datetime

        from .models import ListingViewModel

        lid = listing_id if isinstance(listing_id, _uuid.UUID) else _uuid.UUID(str(listing_id))
        uid = user_id if isinstance(user_id, _uuid.UUID) else _uuid.UUID(str(user_id))
        v = ListingViewModel(listing_id=lid, user_id=uid, viewed_at=datetime.now())
        self.session.add(v)
        await self.session.commit()

    async def get_listing_stats(self, listing_id) -> dict:
        import uuid as _uuid

        from .models import ListingViewModel

        lid = listing_id if isinstance(listing_id, _uuid.UUID) else _uuid.UUID(str(listing_id))
        total = await self.session.execute(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(ListingViewModel)
            .where(ListingViewModel.listing_id == lid)
        )
        unique = await self.session.execute(
            select(func.count(func.distinct(ListingViewModel.user_id))).where(
                ListingViewModel.listing_id == lid
            )
        )
        return {"views": int(total.scalar_one()), "unique_viewers": int(unique.scalar_one())}

    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),

    repo: ListingsRepositoryImpl = Depends(get_listings_repo),

================================================================================
FILE: app/modules/listings/presentation/router.py
================================================================================
"""listings API router."""

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, status
from fastapi import status as http_status

from app.shared.application.side_effects import write_audit
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ConflictDomainError, NotFoundDomainError
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import get_db_session
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_current_user
from app.shared.presentation.authorization import (
    current_user_id,
    is_platform_admin,
    membership_company_ids,
    require_company_admin,
    require_company_member,
)
from app.shared.presentation.error_schemas import error_responses

from ..application.services import ListingsService
from ..domain.entities import ListingsEntity
from .dependencies import get_listings_service
from .schemas import (
    ListingCreateRequest,
    ListingData,
    ListingResponse,
    ListingStatsResponse,
    ListingUpdateRequest,
    PaginatedListingsResponse,
    PaginationMeta,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/listings", tags=["listings"])


def _to_listing_data(entity: ListingsEntity) -> ListingData:
    return ListingData(
        id=entity.id,
        title=entity.title,
        materialType=entity.material_type,
        quantity=entity.quantity,
        unit=entity.unit,
        price=entity.price,
        currency=entity.currency,
        condition=entity.condition,
        location=entity.location,
        status=entity.status,
        companyId=entity.company_id,
        description=entity.description,
        imageUrl=getattr(entity, "image_url", None),
        createdAt=entity.created_at,
        updatedAt=entity.updated_at,
    )


def _membership_company_ids(current_user) -> list:
    """Company IDs clenstva (stringova forma) — jedina zdielana definicia."""
    return membership_company_ids(current_user)


@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=PaginatedListingsResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def list_listings(
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    pageSize: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=100),
    limit: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1, le=100, include_in_schema=False),
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    effective_page_size = limit or pageSize
    company_ids = _membership_company_ids(current_user)
    items = await service.list_all(page, effective_page_size, company_ids=company_ids)
    total = await service.count_all(company_ids=company_ids)
    return PaginatedListingsResponse(
        items=[_to_listing_data(e) for e in items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=effective_page_size, total=total),
    )


@router.get(
    "/marketplace",
    response_model=PaginatedListingsResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def list_marketplace(
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    pageSize: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=100),
    limit: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1, le=100, include_in_schema=False),
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    effective_page_size = limit or pageSize
    company_ids = _membership_company_ids(current_user)
    items = await service.list_marketplace(
        page, effective_page_size, exclude_company_ids=company_ids
    )
    total = await service.count_marketplace(exclude_company_ids=company_ids)
    return PaginatedListingsResponse(
        items=[_to_listing_data(e) for e in items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=effective_page_size, total=total),
    )


@router.get(
    "/matches",
    response_model=PaginatedListingsResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def list_matches(
    materialType: str,
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    pageSize: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=100),
    limit: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1, le=100, include_in_schema=False),
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    effective_page_size = limit or pageSize
    company_ids = _membership_company_ids(current_user)
    items = await service.find_matches(
        material_type=materialType,
        exclude_company_ids=company_ids,
        limit=effective_page_size * page,
    )
    start = (page - 1) * effective_page_size
    slice_items = items[start : start + effective_page_size]
    return PaginatedListingsResponse(
        items=[_to_listing_data(e) for e in slice_items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=effective_page_size, total=len(items)),
    )


@router.post(
    "/{id}/view",
    status_code=http_status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 500),
)
async def record_listing_view(
    id: str,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    """Zapise jedno zobrazenie ponuky prihlasenym userom. Pouziva frontend pri otvoreni detailu."""
    uid = current_user_id(current_user)
    if uid is None:
        return None
    await service.record_view(id, uid)
    return None


@router.get(
    "/{id}/stats",
    response_model=ListingStatsResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 500),
)
async def get_listing_stats(
    id: str,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    """Vrati pocet zobrazeni a unikatnych divakov. Len pre vlastnika."""
    company_ids = _membership_company_ids(current_user)
    stats = await service.get_stats(id, owner_company_ids=company_ids)
    return ListingStatsResponse(**stats)


@router.get(
    "/{id}",
    response_model=ListingResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 500),
)
async def get_listing(
    id: str,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    entity = await service.get_by_id(id)
    if entity is None:
        raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {id} not found")

    # Neverejny (nepublikovany) inzerat vidi len jeho firma alebo platform admin.
    is_public = str(getattr(entity.status, "value", entity.status)) == "published"
    if not is_public and not is_platform_admin(current_user):
        require_company_member(current_user, entity.company_id, action="access")

    return ListingResponse(data=_to_listing_data(entity))


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=ListingResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def create_listing(
    request: ListingCreateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    require_company_member(current_user, request.companyId, action="create listings for")
    entity = ListingsEntity(
        title=request.title,
        material_type=request.materialType,
        quantity=request.quantity,
        unit=request.unit,
        price=request.price,
        currency=request.currency,
        condition=request.condition,
        location=request.location,
        company_id=str(request.companyId),
        description=request.description,
        image_url=getattr(request, "imageUrl", None),
    )
    created = await service.create(entity)
    return ListingResponse(data=_to_listing_data(created))


@router.patch(
    "/{id}",
    response_model=ListingResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def update_listing(
    id: str,
    request: ListingUpdateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    existing = await service.get_by_id(id)
    if existing is None:
        raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {id} not found")

    require_company_admin(current_user, existing.company_id)

    if request.status is not None and request.status.value == "published":
        raise ConflictDomainError("Listing publication must use POST /api/v1/listings/{id}/publish")

    updated_entity = ListingsEntity(
        title=request.title if request.title is not None else existing.title,
        material_type=request.materialType
        if request.materialType is not None
        else existing.material_type,
        quantity=request.quantity if request.quantity is not None else existing.quantity,
        unit=request.unit if request.unit is not None else existing.unit,
        price=request.price if request.price is not None else existing.price,
        currency=request.currency if request.currency is not None else existing.currency,
        condition=request.condition if request.condition is not None else existing.condition,
        location=request.location if request.location is not None else existing.location,
        status=request.status if request.status is not None else existing.status,
        company_id=existing.company_id,
        description=request.description
        if request.description is not None
        else existing.description,
        image_url=request.imageUrl
        if getattr(request, "imageUrl", None) is not None
        else getattr(existing, "image_url", None),
    )
    result = await service.update(id, updated_entity)
    return ListingResponse(data=_to_listing_data(result))


@router.post(
    "/{id}/publish",
    response_model=ListingResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def publish_listing(
    id: str,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
    session=Depends(get_db_session),
):
    existing = await service.get_by_id(id)
    if existing is None:
        raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {id} not found")
    require_company_admin(current_user, existing.company_id)

    try:
        result = await service.publish(id)
    except NotFoundDomainError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        msg = str(exc)
        if "verified" in msg.lower() or "publikovať" in msg.lower():
            raise ConflictDomainError(msg) from exc
        raise

    try:
        actor = current_user_id(current_user)
        await write_audit(
            session=session,
            actor_user_id=actor,
            action="listing.published",
            target_type="listing",
            target_id=id,
            details=f"title={getattr(result, 'title', '')}",
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

    return ListingResponse(data=_to_listing_data(result))


@router.delete(
    "/{id}",
    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 500),
)
async def delete_listing(
    id: str,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: ListingsService = Depends(get_listings_service),
):
    existing = await service.get_by_id(id)
    if existing is None:
        raise NotFoundDomainError(f"Listing {id} not found")
    require_company_admin(current_user, existing.company_id)
    await service.delete(id)

"""Local file upload API (dev-friendly; later swap to MinIO)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import mimetypes
import os
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, HTTPException, UploadFile, status
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse

from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_current_user
from app.shared.presentation.error_schemas import error_responses

from .schemas import UploadResponse

router = APIRouter(prefix="/uploads", tags=["uploads"])

APP_DATA_DIR = os.getenv("APP_DATA_DIR", "/var/data")
UPLOAD_ROOT = Path(APP_DATA_DIR) / "uploads"

ALLOWED_EXT = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".gif", ".pdf"}
ALLOWED_MIME = {
    ".jpg": {"image/jpeg"},
    ".jpeg": {"image/jpeg"},
    ".png": {"image/png"},
    ".webp": {"image/webp"},
    ".gif": {"image/gif"},
    ".pdf": {"application/pdf"},
}
MAX_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
CHUNK_BYTES = 64 * 1024


def _validate_file_id(file_id: str) -> str:
    safe = Path(file_id).name
    if safe != file_id or ".." in file_id or "/" in file_id or "\\" in file_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid file id")

    suffix = Path(safe).suffix.lower()
    stem = Path(safe).stem
    if suffix not in ALLOWED_EXT:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported file extension")
    if len(stem) != 32 or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in stem.lower()):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid file id format")
    return safe


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=UploadResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=error_responses(400, 401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def upload_file(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
):
    if not file.filename:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing filename")

    suffix = Path(file.filename).suffix.lower()
    if suffix not in ALLOWED_EXT:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=f"Unsupported type {suffix}. Allowed: {sorted(ALLOWED_EXT)}",
        )

    if file.content_type:
        allowed_for_ext = ALLOWED_MIME.get(suffix, set())
        if allowed_for_ext and file.content_type not in allowed_for_ext:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=400,
                detail=f"Content type {file.content_type} does not match extension {suffix}",
            )

    UPLOAD_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    file_id = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{suffix}"
    dest = UPLOAD_ROOT / file_id

    total = 0
    try:
        with dest.open("wb") as out:
            while True:
                chunk = await file.read(CHUNK_BYTES)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                total += len(chunk)
                if total > MAX_BYTES:
                    raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="File too large (max 8MB)")
                out.write(chunk)
    except HTTPException:
        dest.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    except Exception:
        dest.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    finally:
        await file.close()

    return UploadResponse(
        fileId=file_id,
        url=f"/api/v1/uploads/{file_id}",
        originalName=file.filename,
        size=total,
        contentType=file.content_type,
    )


@router.get(
    "/{file_id}",
    responses=error_responses(400, 404, 500),
)
async def get_uploaded_file(file_id: str):
    safe = _validate_file_id(file_id)
    path = UPLOAD_ROOT / safe
    if not path.is_file():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="File not found")

    media_type, _ = mimetypes.guess_type(str(path))
    filename = path.name

    response = FileResponse(
        path,
        media_type=media_type or "application/octet-stream",
        filename=filename,
    )
    response.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "private, max-age=3600"
    response.headers["Content-Security-Policy"] = "default-src 'none'; sandbox"
    return response


@router.get(
    "/files/{file_id}",
    include_in_schema=False,
)
async def get_uploaded_file_legacy(file_id: str):
    return await get_uploaded_file(file_id=file_id)

"""uploads API schemas."""

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class UploadResponse(BaseModel):
    fileId: str = Field(..., description="Internal file identifier")
    url: str = Field(..., description="Authenticated retrieval URL")
    originalName: str = Field(..., description="Original uploaded filename")
    size: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="Stored file size in bytes")
    contentType: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Detected or supplied content type")


class UploadErrorResponse(BaseModel):
    error_code: str
    message: str
    details: dict | list | str | None = None
    request_id: str | None = None
